--- Page 11 ---
<meta>This page follows the title page of Sabathier's work. Having established the publication details, the author now presents a formal dedication and theological preface. He addresses Jesus Christ directly, framing his philosophical project—the "Ideal Shadow of General Wisdom"—as a devotional act rooted in the Trinity and the mystery of the Crucifixion.</meta>

<insert>
# TO JESUS CHRIST THE CRUCIFIED
## A WHOLE BURNT OFFERING <note>original: "Holocautoma," a sacrifice entirely consumed by fire, symbolizing the author’s total dedication of his intellect to God.</note>
</insert>

FROM WHERE, I pray, is it more auspicious to begin, than FROM YOU! Since it is safer to proceed THROUGH YOU! And where at last could this **IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM** <note>The book's title; "Shadow" refers to the idea that human knowledge is but a dim reflection of divine truth.</note> more seasonably end, than IN YOU, O CRUCIFIED GOD? FROM WHOM, THROUGH WHOM, and IN WHOM ALL THINGS ARE! FROM YOU, God the Proposer, the First of Actors, the source of *Active Reason*; THROUGH YOU, the splendid **WORD OF GOD**, the source of *Objective Reason*; IN YOU, the Purest **SPIRIT**, the fountain and Primitive Origin of *Unitive Reason*. FROM YOU, the single Root of Indivision; THROUGH YOU, all the Radiance of total Intelligibility; IN YOU, the fuel of Lovability. FROM YOU comes the Integrity of Greatness; THROUGH YOU, the perennity of Duration; IN YOU, the Fullness of Perfection. FROM YOU all things are led forth from the profound Emptiness of Nothingness; THROUGH YOU, they are decorated with the Elegance of their Species and Forms; all things are destined to have their end and Consummation IN YOU. FROM YOU is Power; THROUGH YOU is Wisdom; OUTSIDE OF YOU, You do not Love; WITHOUT WHOM nothing can exist. BEYOND WHOM there is nothing but a figment; OUTSIDE OF YOU, nothing but horror: thus, therefore, FROM YOU were all things given their Essences; thus in reality THROUGH YOU were individual things endowed with their Existences; and thus IN YOU were ALL THINGS established upon the foundations of their Subsistence. And so at last, FROM YOU, THROUGH YOU, and IN YOU, all established things are concluded within the limits of their Consistency.

But FROM WHERE, I ask, do all Created things generally have their Being—FROM YOU, THROUGH YOU, and IN YOU!—unless it is because, more certainly than anything else, **YOU ARE**: since you ARE FROM Yourself, THROUGH Yourself, and IN Yourself most sufficient; that is, BEING ITSELF IN ITS VERY ESSENCE <note>original: "Ipsum Esse Ipsissimum," a scholastic term for God as the ultimate reality.</note>, the Apex of Reasons, the Root of Essences, the *Hypostasis* <term>Hypostasis: The underlying substance or fundamental reality of a thing.</term> and Conclusion of things? And therefore YOU ARE UNITY ITSELF, and TRUTH ITSELF, and GOODNESS ITSELF. In a single Word, singularly and most universally, YOU ARE ALL: will not the beasts of the field teach me this when I doubt? Will the birds of the sky indicate it? Will the earth respond? And will the fish of the sea tell of it? And truly, does not **YOUR REVELATION**, the incorruptible Bringer of Sublime Truths, thunder this very thing—which surpasses all sense and reason—higher than all human logic to all who have ears! While through the mouth of the Beloved Disciple it flashes forth: *In the beginning was the WORD, and the WORD was WITH GOD, and GOD was the WORD: All things were made THROUGH HIM, and WITHOUT HIM nothing was made:* <note>Quoting the opening of the Gospel of John.</note> and did YOU not most openly speak that Great Secret, the Center and Epitome of universal Wisdom, with your own mouth to the trembling MOSES, when he asked who he should preach You to be to rebellious Egypt: **I AM WHO I AM!** <note>original: "EGO SUM QUI SUM," the divine name revealed in Exodus 3:14.</note> So that from that moment, not a single one of your people could be ignorant of the fact that this Name is YOURS; this is your Title; and, if it may be said, This is Your Portion in the Region of Essences, which, as it fits no other, so can it never be communicated to any other at all.

Now, then, is there anything from which **OUR SHADOW** might better begin than FROM YOU, the MOST FULL Image of DIVINITY! By what path might **OUR IDEA** more safely walk than THROUGH YOU, the Mirror without stain of the Majesty of GOD; and where might **GENERAL WISDOM** more happily finish its course than IN YOU, the Lord of Sciences; in Whom are hidden all the treasures of WISDOM and KNOWLEDGE: You, the King of the Universe: who, being One, contain All things, so that they cannot be presented anywhere more perfectly for consideration. Therefore, I thought it right to place You uniquely—as the one who will rule over this whole work of our Diagram—openly in the middle, Exalted on the gibbet of the **CROSS**, so that when all things are drawn TO YOU YOURSELF, as they were led forth FROM YOU YOURSELF, by this universal reduction and derivation, the whole may savor of You; for without YOU, the whole would be utterly foolish: for we shall see both your DARKNESS in your LIGHT, and likewise your LIGHT shining in the Darkness. Thus Your Day, more serene than our utterly Shadowy Day, will finally utter the one **WORD** for which we pant, hidden in the darkness of the FLESH at the feet of the CROSS; and thus your wonderfully Illuminating Night will opportunely show the **SCIENCE OF SALVATION**, which we pursue as lovable above all else, to our night which is deeply dark within.

Only You—whose I burn to be entirely—grant that I may be Yours to such an extent, until, supported by the sincerity of **YOUR FAITH**, the whole may be built upon a single foundation; nor allow me to depart even a fingernail's breadth from the rectitude of **YOUR WILL**, by which the whole must be directed. Therefore, just as I wish all this to be entirely dedicated and consecrated TO YOU, so I wish it to stand subject to the censures of **YOUR CHURCH**, the Catholic and Roman, true and unblemished. In the meantime, O **BLOODY SPOUSE** of Chosen Minds, prostrate at your feet nailed to the stake, I as a suppliant pray and ask: that the Purple Dew of this sacrifice—which is a dew of light and love—and those saving Drops of the Night, with which not only your Curls and Head, but your whole Body is soaked upon the CROSS, may drip so much upon our **IDEAL SHADOWS**, that, pregnant with your LIGHT, they may break forth everywhere into rational Sparks of **Knowledge** of You: Which, flying all around the globe, may so happily entice Men—who are already itching with a natural desire for knowing—to the Understanding of this **IDEAL WISDOM**, so that a frequent encounter with this reading in YOU, **CRUCIFIED JESUS**, and a right **KNOWLEDGE** of YOU alone above all others, may gently insinuate itself into the preoccupied minds of UNBELIEVERS; and may more effectively inspire a sincere LOVE in the now-languid hearts of CHRISTIANS; and finally may inflame ALL people, wherever they may be, with that Sacred FIRE, which You wished to be sent upon the Earth and vehemently kindled. **DO THIS, O GOD HIMSELF.** This is the goal: these are the prayers: for this is **ETERNAL LIFE, THAT THEY MAY KNOW YOU, THE ONLY TRUE GOD, OUR LORD JESUS CHRIST, AND HIM CRUCIFIED.** <note>A paraphrase of John 17:3 combined with Paul’s focus on the crucifixion.</note> Breathe upon these prayers, Favor these designs. BE, I pray, O FORSAKEN GOD, Yourself the one who Favors Your own cause.

<summary>A deeply devotional preface dedicating the entire work to Jesus Christ. The author frames human knowledge (the "Shadow") as dependent on the Divine Word and the "Science of Salvation" found in the Crucifixion.</summary>
<keywords>Jesus Christ, Trinity, Creation, I Am Who I Am, Book of Nature, Wisdom and Knowledge, Crucifixion, Catholic Church, Eternal Life, Theological Philosophy</keywords>

--- Page 14 ---
<meta>This page is a formal dedication to the Pope, likely Innocent XI or XII, serving as a preface to a scholarly work. It establishes the philosophical theme of the book: the pursuit of "Universal Wisdom" and the unification of all knowledge under the authority of the Church.</meta>

# To the Supreme Pontiff of Christians
## A Gesture of Reverence.

*I do not suppose, MOST HOLY FATHER, that <term>whole burnt offerings</term> <note>original: "Holocautomata"; a reference to ancient sacrificial rites.</note> or Altars should be multiplied, when I undertake to bring my Vows from Heaven to the Vatican, from CHRIST to the VICAR of CHRIST. For CHRISTIAN PIETY shrinks from a multiplicity of Vows upon its Altars; the UNITY of Religion, preserving us on both sides, eliminates everything except the One Altar of CHRIST and the CHURCH—over which You preside—and this same Vow of mine. I therefore present again one single Gift to that same Altar, offering to YOU what has already been offered to CHRIST, as a continuous gift of devotion. Indeed, if I look upon YOU with the eyes I ought, I see both YOU in CHRIST and CHRIST in YOU, contemplating you in that bond of necessity which—whether in your Dignity or your Person—exhibits and hands over whatever obedience can be offered to CHRIST himself, the foundation of your high office and the Parent of Apostolic Virtue. For in HIM, YOU are Most Holy, YOU are Greatest, YOU are the Highest. In You, indeed, is the supreme Power, the inexhaustible Virtue, and the faithful Wisdom; and this is so because there is but ONE WHO IS HIS VICAR on Earth. As for the rest of YOU, which is but a Man; yet you are the Man of CHRIST, and nonetheless His VICAR. He who is indeed entirely for CHRIST, and in WHOM CHRIST is found to Live as ONE. You are seen to live as ONE in CHRIST through extraordinary Charity, by a power that translates the Mind into GOD; while in YOU, CHRIST lives through those expressions of His virtues which, shining from the sublime height of your Dignity, strike the eyes of even an unwilling World. Thus, by the holiness of both Person and Office, you are shown to be so entirely of CHRIST on both counts, that whoever honors the PONTIFF with equal honor must also revere INNOCENT <note>A reference to the reigning Pope, likely Innocent XI or XII.</note> with devotion. Indeed, since it is clear that nothing of YOURS is not HIS, I consider it the same whether I venerate INNOCENT in CHRIST, or CHRIST in INNOCENT. Therefore, Holy Father, I believe for certain that I am making an offering to the same Altars and the same DIVINITY, and that I am continuing, not repeating, my offering to CHRIST; while I offer to YOUR HOLINESS the same WORK already initiated for His glory, which has longed to approach your throne since its very conception. By what instinct this happened, I do not know, unless perhaps it is the instinct of Unity; which, when it reigns in the CHURCH, draws everything that serves it toward the HEAD, as if toward a Citadel. Indeed, this WORK was devoted beforehand to UNITY, and to the UNITY of <term>UNIVERSAL WISDOM</term> <note>Latin: "Sapientia Universalis." A 17th-century ideal of organizing all human knowledge into one unified system.</note>, so that it might recall the multitude of Disciplines—which are scattered as if into fragments—back to ONE, or at least bring the diversity of their Order back to identity. And finally, what is most important, it aims to recall the Minds of Mortals—through those very OBJECTS by which they are usually distracted—to a unity of intention toward that ONE NECESSARY thing, which is clearly the best part of our Purpose. Hence, since that same UNITY of UNIVERSAL WISDOM, which is the entire reason for our Purpose, can be seen to boast of a manifold covenant of similarity: both with the CHURCH entrusted to your care (which is but ONE AND CATHOLIC), and with the PONTIFICAL DIGNITY (implanted in you from Heaven, which is likewise GENERAL and INDIVISIBLE), and even with the Person of Your Holiness (visible to Heaven and Earth, who is SINGULAR in himself, yet perhaps so most sacredly COMMON that the CHRISTIAN WORLD acknowledges you equally as its PARENT and its SUN). Because you owe yourself to each individual just as the Sun does, it cannot be denied that either YOUR LOT shines specially in the condition of this Institution, or its condition shines in Yours. Now truly, if it is said that Nature is so hungry for similar things that it gathers even distant things together by a secret pull, is it any wonder if the instinct of the CHURCH—much wiser than Nature—and its more powerful Virtue, should strongly attract this WORK to the seat of the Most Sacred Empire? Though the work is small and humble in its origin, yet by reason of the UNITY and UNIVERSALITY set before it, and the multiform similarity shown in many things, it recognizes itself as a kinsman and ally in CHRIST because it is in harmony; and having been chosen, it is carried there quite willingly, where it predicts nothing will be lacking of all that it ought to desire. Therefore, it goes Eagerly; nay, it flies from my hands, THIS IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM, Holy Father, conscious of its vow, free from fear, and with secure simplicity. It flies, I say, to the thresholds of the Vatican, if they will be open, to bow itself as humbly as possible at the feet of YOUR HOLINESS for approval. It is so prepared for any outcome that it would be most content—indeed, would hold it as a singular benefit—to be trodden underfoot if it is found to be out of harmony with its primary Purpose. Which, as I think has now been sufficiently suggested, is the observance of UNITY: not so much of human WISDOM in the Minds, as of CHRISTIAN Wisdom in the Hearts—that is, the Spirit of UNITY in the CHURCH, in the WORLD, in Faith, and everywhere and through all things, the promotion of Piety toward CHRIST. Furthermore, if it is found useful in any way, it prophesies for itself a more auspicious fate: that, under the humble title of servitude and most obedient, faithful service, it might be deemed worthy of the most desired gift of the APOSTOLIC BLESSING. By which strength it shall happily grow and multiply, for the UNITY which it serves.*

<summary>This page is a formal dedication to Pope Innocent, presenting a philosophical work on "Universal Wisdom." The author argues that just as the Church provides spiritual unity, this book attempts to unify all human knowledge under a single, God-centered framework.</summary>
<keywords>Pope Innocent, Universal Wisdom, Church Unity, Vatican, Philosophy, Apostolic Blessing, Unification of Knowledge</keywords>

--- Page 15 ---
<meta>This page continues the dedication to the Pope, focusing on the philosophical and theological justification for the author's work. It transitions from praising the Pope's person to explaining how the "Universal Wisdom" described in the book mirrors the Unity of the Church and the Papacy itself.</meta>

# To the Supreme Pontiff of Christians
## A Gesture of Reverence.

I do not suppose, MOST HOLY FATHER, that <term>whole burnt offerings</term> <note>original: "Holocautomata"; a reference to ancient sacrificial rites.</note> or Altars should be multiplied, when I undertake to bring my Vows from Heaven to the Vatican, from CHRIST to the <term>VICAR of CHRIST</term> <note>A title for the Pope, indicating he acts as Christ's representative on Earth.</note>. For CHRISTIAN PIETY shrinks from a multiplicity of Vows upon its Altars; the UNITY of Religion, preserving us on both sides, eliminates everything except the One Altar of CHRIST and the CHURCH—over which You preside—and this same Vow of mine. I therefore present again one single Gift to that same Altar, offering to YOU what has already been offered to CHRIST, as a continuous gift of devotion. Indeed, if I look upon YOU with the eyes I ought, I see both YOU in CHRIST and CHRIST in YOU, contemplating you in that bond of necessity which—whether in your Dignity or your Person—exhibits and hands over whatever obedience can be offered to CHRIST himself, the foundation of your high office and the Parent of Apostolic Virtue. For in HIM, YOU are Most Holy, YOU are Greatest, YOU are the Highest. In You, indeed, is the supreme Power, the inexhaustible Virtue, and the faithful Wisdom; and this is so because there is but ONE WHO IS HIS VICAR on Earth. As for the rest of YOU, which is but a Man; yet you are the Man of CHRIST, and nonetheless His VICAR. He who is indeed entirely for CHRIST, and in WHOM CHRIST is found to Live as ONE. You are seen to live as ONE in CHRIST through extraordinary Charity, by a power that translates the Mind into GOD; while in YOU, CHRIST lives through those expressions of His virtues which, shining from the sublime height of Dignity, strike the eyes of the world, even against its will. Thus, both in Person and in the Sanctity of Office, You are revealed as so entirely belonging to CHRIST on both sides, that anyone ought to honor the PONTIFF with no less respect and worship **INNOCENT** <note>The text refers to Pope Innocent (likely Innocent XI or XII).</note> with such observance. Indeed, since it is clear that nothing of YOURS is not also HIS, I think it the same whether I venerate **INNOCENT** in **CHRIST**, or **CHRIST** in **INNOCENT**. Therefore, I truly believe, Blessed Father, that I am making an offering to the same Altar and DIVINITY, and that I am continuing, not repeating, my offering to **CHRIST**; while I offer to YOUR SANCTITY this WORK, already initiated for His glory, which has desired to approach your throne from its very conception. 

By what instinct this came about, I do not know, unless perhaps it is the instinct of Unity; for when Unity reigns supreme in the CHURCH, it powerfully draws toward the HEAD—as if to a Citadel—everything that serves it in any way. Indeed, this WORK was previously dedicated to UNITY and to the UNITY of **UNIVERSAL WISDOM**, so that it might recall the Multitude of Disciplines (which are currently scattered as if into fragments) back to ONE; or at least bring a diversity of order toward identity. And finally—what is most important—it seeks to bring the minds of Mortals, through those very OBJECTS by which they are usually distracted, back to a unity of intention toward that **ONE NECESSARY THING** <note>original: "NECESSARIUM illud UNUM"; a reference to the Gospel of Luke 10:42, "But one thing is necessary."</note>, which is clearly the best part of our Institute. Since this UNITY, which is the **UNIVERSAL WISDOM** and the entire reason for our Institute, can be seen to boast of many similar bonds: both with the CHURCH entrusted to your care, which is but ONE and CATHOLIC; and with the PONTIFICAL DIGNITY bestowed upon YOU from Heaven, which is likewise GENERAL and INDIVISIBLE; and even with the Person of your Holiness himself, visible to Heaven and Earth, who is SINGULAR in yourself, yet perhaps most sacredly COMMON to all, so that the CHRISTIAN WORLD acknowledges YOU as its PARENT, just as NATURE recognizes the SUN as its own; for YOU owe yourself to EACH person as the One. 

It cannot be denied that either YOUR LOT is reflected in the condition of this Institute, or the condition of this Institute shines forth specially in YOURS. For if, as they say, Nature is so greedy for similar things that it openly gathers even distant things by some secret pull, is it any wonder if an instinct far more sagacious than Nature, and a more powerful Virtue, should powerfully attract this WORK to the seat of the Most Sacred Empire? Though small and humble from its origin, this work (by reason of both the UNITY and UNIVERSALITY it aims for, and the many-sided similarity it shows) I dare say is related and akin to **CHRIST**, because it is in harmony with Him. It is carried there electively and not unwillingly, where it foretells that nothing will be lacking to it of all that it ought to desire. 

It goes, therefore, with alacrity; indeed, this **IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM** <note>original: "HÆC IDEALIS UMBRA SAPIENTIÆ GENERALIS"</note>, Blessed Father, flies from my hands, conscious of its vow and void of fear. With secure simplicity, I say, it flies to the threshold of the Vatican, to bow itself as humbly as possible at the feet of YOUR SANCTITY to be judged. It is so prepared for any outcome that it would be most content—indeed, would count it a singular favor—to be trampled underfoot, especially if it is found to be out of harmony with the Institute. This Work, as I think I have now sufficiently suggested, promotes the observance of UNITY—not so much of human WISDOM in the Mind, but of CHRISTIAN Wisdom in the Heart. That is, it suggests the UNITY of Spirit in the CHURCH and in the WORLD, the Unity of Faith, and everywhere and through all things, Piety toward **CHRIST**. Furthermore, if it is found useful in any way, it already prophesies a safe and more auspicious fate for itself: namely, that under the title of most humble servitude and most obedient and faithful service, it might be deemed worthy of the most desired gift of the APOSTOLIC BLESSING. By this, it shall have the strength, with that UNITY it serves remaining unharmed, to grow happily and be multiplied.

<summary>A formal dedication to the Pope (Innocent) presenting a scholarly work on "Universal Wisdom." The author argues that just as the Church and the Papacy represent unity, his book seeks to unify all human knowledge and direct it toward the single "necessary thing": devotion to Christ.</summary>
<keywords>Pope Innocent, Universal Wisdom, Unity of the Church, Vicar of Christ, religious philosophy, Apostolic Blessing, unification of knowledge</keywords>

--- Page 18 ---
<meta>Continuity note: This page serves as the formal dedication of the work to King Louis XIV. The catchword from the previous page or intended for the next is "accessum" (approach/entry).</meta>

# To the Most Christian of Kings

->Patronage<-

**YOUR MAJESTY**, **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**, once questioned me at Bordeaux regarding a certain recent publication of my Meditations, which was delineated by a single multiform **DIAGRAM**. By some fate, word of this work had reached the Royal Ears; you inquired as to what it might be. I remember answering in an "Areopagitic" style <note>The author refers to a dense, mystical, or scholarly style associated with Dionysius the Areopagite</note>: that it appeared to me to be a **ROYAL METHOD OF STUDYING**, by which, if ever a noble desire should enter **YOUR MAJESTY’S** mind, you might delight in an easy and pleasant hunt, and rejoice in the "capture" of knowledge before the toil of it. Then, the **QUEEN MOTHER** <note>Anne of Austria (1601–1666), mother of Louis XIV</note> of illustrious memory, who happened to be present, asked: "Is there nothing useful or suited to my capacity offered in this work?" To whom I replied: "Clearly so, **MOST CHRISTIAN QUEEN**. There is offered for your Royal Devotion the **CHRIST, KING OF KINGS**, Who Opens and No One Shuts. He is offered hanging on the Cross, and everywhere around him is the **SHELL** <note>original: "testa"</note> of Prayer—seeds that are narrow indeed, but which easily, or rather sweetly and without weariness, unfold into immense harvests of the most pious meditations and affections. Finally, the work offers the guidelines for any human operations, by which the course of this life may be most exactly composed, from the Mountain of **CHRIST** and toward that same Mountain." At this, **BOTH THEIR MAJESTIES** commanded that these things be brought before their eyes; but at that time, the unpolished and uncomely face of the project forbade it. Out of modesty, the work thought it a sin to present itself to Royal eyes before it had found its proper light. **BUT NOW**, what was denied then for lack of clarity, the present day has brought forth—and more besides. This is surely the Multiform **FIGURE**, which out of modesty I have chosen to entitle merely the **IDEAL BOOK OF GENERAL WISDOM**. It is now somewhat more complete, beginning to shine with the first rays of glittering light, and is thus less unprepared for the gazes of **YOUR MAJESTY**. **FURTHERMORE**, there is another more powerful reason that has led my mind to this religious duty of offering my obedience to **YOUR MAJESTY**, **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**. The somewhat condensed breadth of this **SCHEME** of ours testifies that I am, in thought at least, a **SPIRIT** that wanders through all things—indeed, one that returns as well. Let it be clearly so. Behold, my departure is from the highest Heaven, where **CHRIST** sits at the Right Hand of **GOD**; from there, my transit is through the **VATICAN**, where the **VICAR OF CHRIST** <note>The Pope, likely Innocent XI</note> likewise sits in the Chair of Peter; and **NOW** my path runs even to the summit of France, where the **MOST CHRISTIAN OF KINGS** sits upon the Throne of the **KINGDOM**. And in each of these regions, though they are quite distant, I encounter these summits of dignity sitting in their ordered places of honor and power: **CHRIST** in the Heavens, **INNOCENT** in the Church, and **LOUIS** in France. These three are such, and each for his own reason, that they exist on the same Line of Christian Rectitude that I have followed. They are so related and continuous in that very Rectitude that, although they are three, they must be one through the Sacrament of the Divine Number—that unity of Spirit which, despite the diversity of nations, makes the adoration of the Christian Name one and the same, breathing together in the Catholic Truth. For He is the greatest **CHRIST** <note>The author uses "Christ" here in the sense of "Anointed One"</note> who is the same yesterday, today, and forever. But who is more truly a **CHRIST** on earth than the one **VICAR OF CHRIST**? And who in the world is **GREAT** if not he who remains the **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**? Therefore, these three—**CHRIST**, **INNOCENT**, **LOUIS**—remain as one. But the greatest of these is **CHRIST**, who is **HEAD OF THE CHURCH** and **KING** of all. Since these, my **HEADS**, have encountered me so auspiciously, what should stand in the way of my professing the rights of each in this **WORK** of mine? To whom should I first render my vows but to **CHRIST**? And to whom should I show due reverence but to **INNOCENT**, the **VICAR OF CHRIST**? Does not my duty of client-like observance also claim its right from you, **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**? The encounter with **YOUR MAJESTY** is a happy one for me, proceeding as I am along the same unswerving Path of Rectitude. For that path is joined to the unity of both **CHRIST** and the **VICAR OF CHRIST** by the most sacred affinities—of Divine Religion, of Royal Sovereignty, and of the uniting Spirit. Indeed, you are distinguished by both of them with singular prerogatives in that **CHURCH** which joins them both. For if that is the House of **GOD**, **YOU** are the strong armed man guarding His court; if it is a City, **YOU** are the Tower in her, from which hang a thousand shields for her defense; if it is a Ship, **YOU** are the Lighthouse providing a most opportune station during her tossing; if it is a battle line, **YOU** are the French Mars within it; if she is the tamer of monsters (namely Heresies), **YOU** are her Christian Hercules; if you fight for Peace, **YOU** are her **LOUIS THE PEACEFUL**; if she is a flowery field, **YOU** are her **LILY**, the glory and majesty of the flowers; if she is Heaven, **YOU** are the Jupiter within it, the most fortunate star of Religions; if she is the Mother of the faithful, **YOU** are her **ELDEST SON**; and if finally that **CHURCH** is the **KINGDOM OF CHRIST**, then in it, by the Grace of **GOD** Himself, **YOU** are the **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**. Therefore, I approach **LOUIS THE GREAT** along the same straight path I take toward **CHRIST**, the supreme **HEAD OF THE CHURCH**, and toward **INNOCENT**, the supreme **PONTIFF OF THE CHURCH**, to return to him the rights belonging to his many Names and Titles. And so, **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**, I offer to **YOUR MAJESTY** this small "Head" of my labor, intending to work hereafter on the formation of the rest of the "Body," as the Grace of the **DIVINE POWER** and the favorable breeze of **ROYAL MAJESTY** shall inspire me. I hope for happy auspices, which the horoscope of this **WORK** seems to promise me with no uncertainty, being most fortunate under the benign aspect of the **FRENCH MARS** <note>A reference to the King's military prowess</note>. Having immediately changed a warlike mind into a Peaceful one to reconcile Heaven with the Earth, and being content with the most rapid course of your Victories—which the entire massed crowd of confederated enemies could nowhere stop—it was only right that **LOUIS THE GREAT** should be conquered by **LOUIS THE GREAT** alone, so that he might give **PEACE TO EUROPE** in our days. This promises an opportune and not entirely unwelcome reception for this First Part of the **WORK** (which I can rightly call a Work of Peace, conceived during the Peace of the Pyrenees <note>1659 treaty between France and Spain</note> and published during the Peace of Nijmegen <note>1678–1679 treaties ending the Franco-Dutch War</note>). It promises a favorable progress into the same realms of light for the parts to follow, to the greater glory of **HIM FROM WHOM, THROUGH WHOM, AND IN WHOM** all things exist. Live for **HIM**, **O KING**, and for **YOUR OWN** forever.

<summary>A formal dedication to King Louis XIV of France, framing the author's philosophical and educational system (the "Ideal Book of General Wisdom") as a tool for royal study and a reflection of the divine order connecting Christ, the Pope, and the French Monarchy.</summary>
<keywords>Louis XIV, Louis the Great, Pope Innocent XI, Peace of Nijmegen, Peace of the Pyrenees, Royal Method of Study, General Wisdom, Most Christian King, Christology, Political Theology</keywords>

# PREFACE

->Why do you linger at the threshold, OBSERVER, whoever you are, before THIS DIAGRAM!<- Why, just as you are about to penetrate the inner secrets of WISDOM, do you pull back your foot so quickly in astonishment! Did an unexpected encounter with a **SHADOW** <note>original: "UMBRAE"</note> deter you from entering, or does it hold your mind in suspense? Is there a believable partnership between Darkness and LIGHT, between WISDOM <note>original: "SAPIENTIAE"</note> and the Shadows which she brings forth by her GAZE? Behold, He who placed his tabernacle in the Sun, also made darkness his hiding place. Indeed, from the awareness of how small our own clarity is in this—albeit grand—task, we mark our attempt in what we have tried to sketch in such a SCHEME with the TITLE you see; we do this so that it may profess on its very front both the magnitude of the subject itself and the narrowness of our own poverty.

Does your mind go numb at the sight of the mixture of CHRISTIAN SECRETS with JEWISH ones under a foreign character, suspecting something foul? Far from it. There lies hidden a most holy Covenant of Religion, by which that tradition of the JEWS—no less sacred than it is secret—which they call the **KABBALAH** <note>A Jewish mystical tradition that interprets the Bible through esoteric methods.</note>, is proved to agree with our own MYSTERIES in CHRIST.

Finally, does such a jumble of FIGURES and ICONS, the variation of COLORS, and the entanglement of CIRCLES and revolving ORBS present the appearance of some Inextricable LABYRINTH! A discerning mind, having caught the scent of its errors, twists, and turns, and loathing the labor within, might not know how to enter, finding neither a way to penetrate nor an exit to return. Cast off your fear; it is a groundless panic. Look at the TITLE; look at its SUPPORTS <note>original: "TALAMONES", referring to the architectural figures supporting the structure of the diagram.</note>. With WISDOM as the Guide and VIRTUE as the Companion, what is there to fear!

*Wisdom, being thus One, can do All things.*
If that is not enough: *Behold, United Virtue Triumphs over everything.*
Add to this that, JUST AS THE CHURCH IS IN WISDOM, so in VIRTUE
The Firstborn Bride of CHRIST,
*From All the Flowers of the World, You have chosen for Yourself the One Lily.* <note>A reference to France, symbolized by the fleur-de-lis.</note>
And Just as HERE THE TRIAD IS UNITY;

<column-break/>

In Wisdom, indeed, the Spirit of Understanding is Unique for Seeing.
In Virtue, furthermore, the Spirit of Union is Effective for Acting.
May you have FRANCE <note>original: "GALLIAM"</note> displayed. For you, Noble France, are stainless.
But that other; Truth, widely One, the One Contemplatrix, has taken the POMEGRANATE <note>original: "MALOGRANATUM"; a traditional symbol of the unity of the Church or the many parts of wisdom contained in one whole.</note> for her hieroglyphic Type.
So THERE the TRIAD; unity Crowns forever.

<column-break/>

What room is there now, I ask you, for fear! What should you dread under such great protections! Call upon WISDOM; Call upon VIRTUE—the WISDOM OF GOD and the VIRTUE OF GOD, which I understand to be CHRIST. WISDOM will lead you on a wondrous Way; VIRTUE will equip you with the Helmet of salvation. Call additionally upon the CHURCH: She will defend you with the Sword of the Word. Call out to FRANCE: she will protect you with the Shield of Justice. Be of good cheer and courage: and together with me, having first taken up the wise thread—not of Ariadne, but of CHRIST—do not hesitate to enter our LABYRINTH. HE will perfect your steps in His paths, so that your footsteps may not falter. Nay, you shall see and marvel how Light shines out of darkness within it: and I believe you will perceive a hope arising that you may reach the recognition and KNOWLEDGE OF THE TRUTH much more easily, quickly, and surely by this way than is usual.

But so that experience may prove the faith of the matter: let us enter, if you please. First, with my mind opened to you, and the Purpose and Occasion of the Work revealed, let us run through the space with a light and swift step, just as befits a FORERUNNER and as an INTRODUCTION prescribes; until it is permitted to unfold everything one by one with a fuller explanation of things.

---

## AN ISAGOGIC FORERUNNER INTO THE IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM.

The INCENTIVE and OCCASION for sketching this general SYSTEM of ours, HUMAN OBSERVER, must soon be taken from its very MARGIN. For nothing among those things it supports is silent, as they are heavy with the weight of many meanings; rather, everything there speaks Kabbalistically, prepared equally for the BEGINNING as a PRELUDE and for the end as an EPILOGUE, so that they may respond with the same ease when called to either duty. As a PRELUDE, therefore, we display this MARGIN divided into four parts, like the usual parts of our WORLD: namely, the EAST, the WEST, the SOUTH, and the NORTH.

In the **HIGHEST REGION**, as if in the deepest NOON of inaccessible Light, the **ONE DIVINITY** is symbolized Kabbalistically by the letter **YOD** <note>The smallest letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often used to represent the name of God or the point of creation.</note> placed there, bright in the center—the simplest of letters among the HEBREWS and the Origin of the others, and therefore wisely claimed by them as a Symbol of DIVINITY.

In the **LOWEST REGION**, as in the NORTH and opposite the first, the **ONE CHURCH** is similarly designated in the center, under the Type of a venerable WOMAN in priestly robes offering incense to the DEITY.

Then on the **RIGHT**, as if from the EAST, the **ONE TRUTH** shines in the center under the symbolic appearance of the SUN.

Finally, on the **LEFT**, as if from the WEST, the **ONE SCIENCE** <note>In this context, "Science" (Scientia) means "Knowledge" or a systematic branch of learning.</note> is represented in a MIRROR reflecting the Sun, as the image of TRUTH. Furthermore, each one of these four things is seen surrounded by a company of its own kind, as the Prince and Standard-bearer in its own region, distinguished by their own Unities: DIVINITY itself has several on the right and left, such as Dignities and Prerogatives...

<summary>This preface introduces a complex mystical diagram that harmonizes Christian theology with Jewish Kabbalah. The author encourages the reader to enter this "labyrinth" of symbols, explaining that the diagram is structured around four cardinal points representing Divinity, the Church, Truth, and Knowledge.</summary>
<keywords>Preface, Kabbalah, Christian Mysticism, Labyrinth, Wisdom, Virtue, France, Symbolism, Jod, Divinity, Church, Truth, Science</keywords>

<meta>This page serves as a preface or introduction to a systematic work, likely an emblem book or a theological treatise. It establishes the central theme of "Unity" (Unitas) across four domains: God, the Church, Truth, and Academic Discipline. The author uses a structured, almost architectural logic to link these concepts together, preparing the reader for a visual "System" explained at the end.</meta>

The prerogatives of the CHURCH, the endowments of TRUTH, and the conditions of KNOWLEDGE <note>original: "SCIENTIÆ"</note>—each unique in its own complexity yet harmoniously One—are all shadowed forth in their respective emblems. Thus the CHURCH carefully preserves the Unity of the Spirit; thus from the Unity of Truth, KNOWLEDGE <note>This refers to systematic learning or science in the classical sense.</note> perfectly coalesces into one whole.

This introductory series of Unities emerged in the quiet of a deep solitude, born from a profound meditation upon the Nature and the DISPOSITION OF THE DIVINE DEITY, especially regarding UNITY. Indeed, in that meditation, it became clear that the Divine dwells in an inaccessible solitude of Light in its MIDDAY—that is, in the highest fullness of HIS DIVINITY. Although He possesses every form of good within Himself, He claims none for Himself except insofar as it is most essentially ONE, both in Himself and with all others. Just as in HIMSELF, so also in the CHURCH (His House), He does not seem to uphold anything except under this character of dignity.

For just as HE HIMSELF, though eminently ALL THINGS, is by His nature so **ONE**, that He exists only as:

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| That by which He is free from change: **A. ONE IMMUTABILITY**, which is the highest NECESSITY of Being; | That by which He prevails over all: **D. ONE VIRTUE**, which is His wonderful OMNIPOTENCE; |
| That by which He stands above all: **B. ONE MAJESTY**, which is His unseen GLORY; | That by which He looks out for each thing: **E. ONE VIGILANCE**, which is His unique PROVIDENCE; |
| That by which He rules all: **C. ONE EMPIRE**, which is His supreme AUTHORITY; | That by which He is sufficient for Himself and others: **F. ONE FULLNESS**, which is His all-encompassing INFINITY. |

(Take care, however, that you do not suspect a "multitude" emerging from so many Unities; by reason of this INFINITY which embraces each one, **SIMPLICITY** perfectly unites them all.)

So too, outside of Himself—so that He might be known and eternally worshipped—He has, by the counsel of His Will, destined the CHURCH to be so much **ONE** for Himself, that His UNITY governs each of her prerogatives; and thus it is established:

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| Whereby she is open to all: **G. ONE GATE**, which is the BAPTISM of Regeneration; | Whereby she is vigilantly governed: **K. ONE HEAD**, who is the most Blessed POPE; |
| Whereby she is enlightened from heaven: **H. ONE LIGHT**, which is the CATHOLIC FAITH IN CHRIST; | Whereby she is more perfectly united: **L. ONE HEART**, which is unanimous CHARITY; |
| Whereby she offers sacrifice to the NAME: **I. ONE SACRIFICE**, which is the venerable EUCHARIST; | Whereby she is continually given life: **M. ONE SPIRIT**, who is the sevenfold COMFORTER <note>original: "PARACLETUS"</note>. |

From this meditation, the thought occurs: could there not also be a single **DISCIPLINE** for TRUTH, which is likewise diffused through individual things and yet equally **ONE**, marked by the Unities of its own endowments? Of Truth, it is said:

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| By which she appears to all: **N. ONE FACE**, which is her native SPLENDOR; | Whereby she eternally persists: **Q. ONE FIRMAMENT**, which is her unshakable SOLIDITY; |
| Whereby she dwells perpetually: **O. ONE ELEMENT**, which is her untainted SINCERITY; | Whereby she is more safely approached: **R. ONE WAY**, which is circumspect TEACHABILITY; |
| Upon which she restfully leans: **P. ONE SEAT**, which is her constant EQUALITY; | Whereby she tastes pleasant: **S. ONE FRUIT**, which is sober WISDOM. |

Could there be, I ask, a single **DISCIPLINE** sufficient for human capacity to track Truth down—one that might perhaps save the time and labor of STUDENTS? Such a discipline would have:

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| That which it focuses on solely: **T. ONE ARGUMENT**, which is the Adequate OBJECT; | Whom it listens to diligently: **V. ONE TEACHER**, who is Wise in all things; |
| That which it examines to the core: **U. ONE MEDIUM**, which is the accurate DEFINITION OF THE THING; | That which it studiously holds to: **Z. ONE ORDER**, which is a uniform METHOD; |
| That which it freely yields to: **X. ONE ARBITRESS**, which is Right REASON; | Where it constantly tends: **Y. ONE GOAL**, which is its PROPER END. |

And indeed, these Unities of the principal things of the UNIVERSE—or, if you prefer, these "Solitudes of Unities"—are expressed by their symbols, as we have noted, and are presented on the very MARGINAL BORDER OF THE FIGURES. Thus, the DESIGN and OCCASION of this illustrated work are signaled and begin right at the threshold.
Truly, the consideration of these things strikes one: that for the greater glory of JESUS CHRIST, and for the sanctification, relief, and shortcut of STUDENTS and STUDIES—under His guidance and light—an ideal specimen of this GENERAL DISCIPLINE to be established has been attempted in outline.
UNDER THIS FIGURE: by which a UNIVERSAL SYSTEM <note>original: "SYSTEMATE"</note> is presented to the eyes as a Catholic [universal] institution; and it is to be considered under a TRIPLE ASPECT in which it appeared to be offered: namely,

| | | | | |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- | :--- |
| | { In its Generality, | CONFUSED, | | NIGHT; |
| **IN** | { In its Parts, | COMPLICATED, | Like | DAWN; |
| | { **CHRIST**, | EXPLICATED; | | DAY. |

---

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| **IN** | **CONFUSION** truly it *Grows Dark* from the lack of distinction among things lying there as if in *CHAOS*; |
| **IN** | **COMPLICATION** indeed it *Dawns* from FAITH, as if the *AURORA* of our knowledge; |
| **IN** | **EXPLICATION** furthermore it *Becomes Day* from REASON; which is here our "DAY," such as it is. |

Of these, those drawn within the **HEAVEN** are taken from the Lowest Figure, while those that shine all around outside the **HEAVEN** are to be taken from its **CENTER**, so that they may be distinguished from one another. **IN NIGHT**, the **CONFUSED UNIVERSE** <note>Latin: *Universitas Confusa*; refers to the world in its chaotic, unformed state</note> remains entirely silent to the gaze of the observer, showing nothing but a face of inaccessible darkness all around. In the Day, however, the **EXPLAINED UNIVERSE** <note>Latin: *Universitas Explicata*; the world as revealed or unfolded</note> escapes or dulls the mortal sight by the excessive brightness of its rays: eyes recently emerging from the darkness of **NIGHT** cannot suddenly endure such a great force of Light, unless they are gradually accustomed, refreshed, and strengthened by a milder and more kindly Light. But when **CHRIST ON THE CROSS** is looked upon to be invoked with the word [**A. P. E. R. I.**] <note>Latin: "Open." Here used as an acronym for the phrase that follows.</note>; for he himself is the one **FROM WHOM, THROUGH WHOM, and IN WHOM ARE ALL THINGS** <note>original: *A QUO, PER QUEM, et IN QUO SUNT OMNIA*</note>; even the **HIDDEN TREASURES OF WISDOM**; He who has the **KEY OF KNOWLEDGE**, **WHO OPENS**, and no one **CLOSES**; who **CLOSES**, and no one opens: or rather, while the **UNIVERSE** itself is more attentively considered as if **ENFOLDED IN THE DAWN** within the **CRUCIFIED JESUS**: these things seem to shine forth as if emerging from the midst of his **BREAST**, and around it his **FOUR** supports, as if they were **PIVOTS** <note>Latin: *Cardines*; literally "hinges" or cardinal points</note>, appear in the extremities of the **PARTS OF THE CRUCIFIED ONE** in this arrangement, namely —

-> **IN THE HEAD, DIRECTION;** <-
-> **IN THE RIGHT HAND, CONSTITUTION;** <-
**IN THE BREAST, THE UNIVERSE;**
-> **IN THE LEFT HAND, DISTRIBUTION;** <-
-> **IN THE FEET, UNIFICATION.** <-

The **UNIVERSE** <note>The Whole</note> is indeed in the Open **BREAST**, as if in the **CENTER OF THE UNIVERSAL ENFOLDING**; **DIRECTION** is in the **HEAD**, as if in the **CITADEL OF LIGHTS** of those who rule: for **HE** is the **TRUE LIGHT**, which illuminates every man coming into this **WORLD**; **CONSTITUTION** is in the **RIGHT HAND**, as if by as many **FINGERS**, so many radiated **ORDERS OF REASONS** <note>Principles or logical structures</note> of the constituting elements.
**DISTRIBUTION** is in the **LEFT HAND**, as if by as many **JOINTS**, so many branching **CLASSES** of things; finally, **UNIFICATION** is in the **FEET**, and these joined by a **NAIL**, as if in a **COLLECTED DECADE** of the ten toes, for the **BINDING** of things into **UNITY, THE STABILIZER OF THE UNIVERSE.**
When these things are then by reason transferred into the darkness of the Abyss, and there studiously unfolded as far as is permitted, the Night might somehow recede, and some Day might finally shine upon the **UNIVERSE**. * And thus the well-counseling **FAITH OF CHRIST**, suggesting **REASON** to the believer in the investigation of things, causes one to be happily initiated by its light as if by a **DAWN**, so that a **CATHOLIC-CHRISTIAN PROCESS OF EXPLORING THE UNIVERSE** might be established, according to its **FOUR** such **CARDINAL POINTS**, to which the remaining things serve, following the series conceived in the **EXTENDED BODY OF CHRIST** to be unfolded thereafter. And so:

I. The **DIRECTION OF THE UNIVERSE** { **CHRIST,** } The **REGIONS** of our **SYSTEM**:
through **LIGHTS**, namely { **and LAWS,** } set forth:
> 1. The **IMAGE** itself of the **SUFFERING CHRIST**, who is through himself the one Reason for universal **DIRECTION**.
> 2. The **RAYS** shining further from the edge of the **FIGURE**; in which are read both the **SYMBOLS OF THE LAWS**, and then the **LAWS OF WISDOM** or **DOGMAS** themselves: a more convenient place for enumerating these individually will occur below.

II. The **CONSTITUTION OF THE UNIVERSE**
From these Five **ORDERS OF REASONS**, namely:
- **Hypostatic** <note>The essential or foundational level</note>,
- **Seminal** <note>The generative or "seed" level</note>,
- **Instructive**,
- **Actuating**,
- **Stative** <note>The stable or fixed level</note>,
is designated by as many partial **FIGURES** which follow.
> 1st. The **EARTHLY TRIANGLE** near the Darkened Globe; in which is the **HYPOSTATIC ORDER OF CONSTITUTING REASONS**: and they are Three in number, namely:

-> **ACTIVITY, —————— THE BOND. —————— PASSIVITY,** <-
-> **IVUM, —————— RE-RI. —————— ILE,** <-
-> ♃ <note>Jupiter</note> —————— ☿ <note>Mercury</note> —————— ♀ <note>Venus</note> <-

2nd. The triple **WATERY SQUARE**; where the **SEMINAL ORDER OF CONSTITUTING REASONS** is found: three of which are as if the Princes and Leaders, as in their Square, Three follow each one as a Leader, absolute,

<meta>This page continues the systematic classification of philosophical and theological principles, specifically detailing the "Seminal Reasons" that govern the structure of the universe.</meta>

And just as many relative principles, wandering thus around the three squares, seeking an entrance from the outside to destroy the treacherous CONSTITUTION, pursuing the PRINCIPLES OF DESTRUCTION. However, the nature and character of all these Reasons, their arrangement and order, their relation and condition, will be indicated by a double series—namely, the Triple-eight <note>Latin: *Trioctona*; a system of 24 principles arranged in three groups of eight.</note> and the Eight-triple—with the help of the following DIAGRAM.

---

### The Triple-eight Series of Seminal Reasons <note>Latin: *Rationum Seminalium*; these are the latent principles or "seeds" of reality that determine how things grow and exist.</note>

<margin>The Eight-triple Series of Seminal Reasons.</margin>

| | 1. Initial Reasons | 2. Middle Reasons | 3. Final Reasons | |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1.** | **A. UNITY.** | **B. TRUTH.** | **C. GOODNESS.** | Objective Reasons. <margin>Absolute Reasons.</margin> |
| **2.** | **D. Power.** | **E. Wisdom.** | **F. Will.** | Potential Reasons. |
| **3.** | **G. Virtue.** | **H. Operation.** | **I. Glory.** | Actual Reasons. |

The final two volumes, or phylacteries <note>religious cases containing scriptural texts</note>, contain several SYMBOLIC FIGURES, by which certain profound mysteries of both DIVINITY and Creation are Kabbalistically designated and thus unlocked.

<image-desc>A radiant triangle containing the Hebrew Tetragrammaton (יהוה) and a central eye-like figure.</image-desc>
1. IT IS THE HOLY SPIRIT OF GOD, prefiguring the Unity of the most holy DIVINITY in the unspeakable majesty of eternity, like a center within its own circle. From Him, through Him, in Him, and toward Him all things exist and return, as the eternal and sacramental PRINCIPLE of all things. This is the FIRST radiance of the DIVINE TRI-UNITY emanating from the depths of eternity, designating the ETERNAL FATHER as the SOURCE OF WISDOM.

<image-desc>A complex diagram with the Hebrew letter Shin (ש) set between two interlocking or adjacent triangles.</image-desc>
2. IT IS THE <term>Protoplast|The original or first-created form</term> SPIRIT of the Universe from the center of the Uncreated Trinity, like a ray from the sun. As a spirit from the Spirit, it grants the understanding of the mysteries of the Word [the "FIAT" or "Let it be done"], while the pledge of God's covenant with Angels and Men is stirred in the Sacrament of the Tongue—or the Knowledge of the Voice—to preach the wonders of Divine Wisdom. This is so that we may all truly be gods and children of the MOST HIGH, born from the Word of GOD through the understanding of the WORD. It is also the first expanse of the Inaccessible Light.

<image-desc>A triangular arrangement of Hebrew letters forming a tetractys of the name Jehovah.</image-desc>
3. It is the Unspeakable NAME OF THE DIVINE TRI-UNITY, a symbolic model of creation engraved by divine hand into every being. It shows the sacramental bond of GOD THE CREATOR with His creatures through the final letter [ת] <note>the Hebrew letter Tav</note> of this holy and terrible NAME. It also sets forth the seventy-two wonders of DIVINE EXISTENCE, and the SON OF THE MIND, or the WORD OF GOD as a RIVER OF INTELLIGENCE.

<image-desc>The Hebrew letters for the Pentagrammaton (יהשוה - Yehoshuah).</image-desc>
4. It is the most holy, mystical, and adorable NAME of the Majesty of the ETERNAL WORD, revealing the glory of His Divinity, Incarnation, Passion, and Resurrection. This is the Name above every name given to men for salvation by the Angel of Great Counsel: the SANCTUARY OF KABBALISTIC TRUTH.

<image-desc>An eight-pointed circular diagram or wheel with a central hub.</image-desc>
5. IT IS THE SUPER-CELESTIAL WATER, or the metaphysical <term>Hyle Mundi|Primordial matter of the world</term> from the Prototype Spirit. It is the Mother of Things, producing the fourfold from the twofold. It is the first principle of division, multitude, disparity, opposition, discord, and contrariety, which finally directs the instrument of its virtue and the power of its motion downward. From this, it individuates specific materials and the bodies of things, giving them existence.

<image-desc>A circular diagram showing three interlocking or concentric paths of flow.</image-desc>
6. It is the circular evolution of the emanations of the Eternal TRI-UNITY over the center of the UNCREATED UNITY. It celebrates the harmony of the world in mystical weight, number, and measure, through which GOD created, saw, numbered, and measured all things through Wisdom. For number consists of order and measure; measure likewise of order and number; and finally, order consists of number and measure. Thus, the Law of Order and the Order of the Law of GOD and Nature is a sacrament to the Immortal Soul, so that His will may be done in Heaven and on Earth.

<meta>This page continues the esoteric exploration of the relationship between the Divine, the celestial bodies, and the composition of the human soul and body, likely following a section on the initial principles of creation.</meta>

<margin>3</margin> The Spirit of God, which is the **HOLY SPIRIT** <note>original: *Spiritus Sanctus*</note> filling the whole world: From this, because it contains All Things, it possesses the Knowledge of the Voice, by which the Heavens declare the glory of **GOD**, and by which every Creature proclaims His presence, praising Him together. Therein is the **RIVER OF KNOWLEDGE**.

<margin>4</margin> <image-desc>ornamental cross symbol</image-desc> It is the hierarchical **MAGNITUDE** of Light, the **HYLIASTER** <note>original: *Hyliastrum*; a Paracelsian term for the primary matter of the stars or elemental fire</note> of Elemental Fire: the Fiery Light directs the acts and phenomena of its Virtue upward as the formal **ALL-WORKER** <note>original: *Panurgon*; from the Greek for 'all-working'</note> of the Sun, Moon, stars, and finally all Life: because, purified by the **ONE**, it is drawn toward the **UNITY** of Uncreated Light, from which it burns.

<margin>5</margin> <image-desc>ornamental flaming lamp or candelabrum</image-desc> It is the Internal Disposition <note>original: *Diathesis*</note>, or the Tri-Une Substance <note>original: *Hypostasis*</note> of **HEAVEN** and **EARTH**, the basis and Center of the formal quaternary of concrete or bodily **ELEMENTS**, from which, through which, and in which all things composed of elements stand, move, and flow back. It is also the **PRIME MATTER** <note>original: *Materia Prima*</note> of the final Bodies or the Ultimate Matter, whose Tri-Une Virtue is the Astral Bond, activating the Celestial Virtues in earthly things and attracting them magnetically. Where the Triad is reduced through the Tetrad <note>the number four</note> to the Simplicity of the monad <note>the number one</note>.

<margin>6</margin> <image-desc>ornamental tree-cross symbol</image-desc> The **KABBALISTIC TREE** of Nature, whose root and life-center, proceeding from the **TRIAD** of Uncreated Light, sends forth three Rays of Life: One from the right, another from the left, both tending toward that place from which they bring the Force and the **HYLEG** <note>original: *Hylech*; an astrological term for the 'giver of life'</note> of the Celestial Zodiac, imitating the Sun; the other, the Middle Ray, radiates straight ahead; from here, uniting the collateral Force of both, it procreates the **NURSERY** of Nature from the multiplicative Virtue of the **ARCHAEUS** <note>the vital formative force in Paracelsian philosophy</note> for the quickening of the flesh of the earthly body through the Virtue of the Word [**LET IT BE** <note>original: *FIAT*</note>], and of His mandate [**BE FRUITFUL and MULTIPLY**]. Finally, it is that mystical **MUD** <note>original: *limus*</note> from which **MAN** was made as a Living Being by **GOD'S** own hand: as a testament to this is the sign **TAV** <note>the last letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often used as a mystical mark</note>, or the Kabbalistic $\triangle$ **Aleph**, otherwise the **HOLY TRIAD**, the **ARCHETYPE** of the Immortal Mind, which **MAN** wore on his forehead before the fall—undivided, enclosed, and joined in the Sacramental **TRISAGION** <note>original: *Trisagio*; the 'thrice-holy' hymn</note> of **DIVINE UNITY**—thus being Immortal. Now, since the fall, it is broken and open, wherefore man, fluctuating at the crossroads of Matter, experiences the discords and contrarieties of the Quaternary (into which he fell), which are the source of disease, corruption, and death. But the Incarnation of **CHRIST** restored it through the Sacrament of regeneration, and His Passion through the glory of the Resurrection.

<margin>7</margin> The Perfection of the whole **WORLD** is in the mystical work of the six days, where the Higher and Lower, East and West, North and South prefigure for the **WORLD** that Hieroglyph of the World which reveals the Seven Lights of the **WORLD** in the Mystery of the Seven days of Creation. For the Center of the Hexad <note>the number six</note> gives the Septenary <note>the number seven</note>, in which Nature rests and revolves, and which **GOD** chose for the Sanctification of His most Holy **NAME**. From the septenary, I say, arises the **LIGHT OF THE WORLD**, because from It the ascent is made to the denary <note>the number ten</note>, which is the Horizon of Eternity. From this comes the Virtue of all things, and all power.

<margin>8</margin> **VIRTUE** belongs to the first Mover of the World, or the astral and perpetual motion of **NATURE**, imitating the perfection and course of the Circle in the perennial Liturgy of generations. For the Heavens, from the fertile treasures of Seminal reasons <note>the seeds of all things</note>, effect the **REBIRTH** <note>original: *Palingenesiam*</note> of Individuals; so that as Stewards, from the firmament of their Seeds through the great Ascendant of the Great Monarchy of the **WORLD**, they might utterly emulate the eternity from which they come.

<margin>9</margin> It is the **UNIVERSAL SPIRIT** of the World, all-powerful in Virtue, diffusing from the **AZOTH** <note>The 'universal solvent' or 'mercury of the wise' in alchemy</note> or the **SOUL of the WORLD** the seminal reasons and Vital rays into all things, so that the harmony of the world's economy is preserved; through It all things are renewed and regenerated from the Circumference of the World to the Center, and from the center back to the surface or the Face of Matter, leading and bringing back the Seminal Virtues, binding the Lowest to the Highest and celestial things to earthly ones. Thus generation is the approach of the **AZOTH**, or otherwise the flow of Seeds.

<margin>10</margin> It is the **INDIVIDUAL** existence of any bodily thing outside of its **STATE**, standing in its own Being or Cube, perfect and consisting of the four Vital Powers of the **ELEMENTS**, from which it is nourished, grows, and is preserved in many ways in its bodily being. Four, I say, are the Mothers of natural things, namely the **ELEMENTS**, from which they proceed and to which they return when mixed together. Thus all death and corruption is the withdrawal of **AZOTH**, or the Ebb of Seeds—likewise applied as follows.

Beneath all these things, this Sphere of the Divine World also has its own **THEOLOGICAL ROSARY OF GLORY** with an **ALPHABET**.

### The 4th FIGURE of DISTRIBUTION is the BLUE CIRCLE of VOLUMES; in which is the PARTICULAR DISTRIBUTION of the UNIVERSE (and the last of Natural things) into SINGULARITIES: not indeed every single thing, the number of which is inaccessible to man, but the more distinguished things, recorded by a peculiar method in the Monuments of History. The headings of these are arranged there in a nearly Cosmic series.

## 4th Circle, the Boundary of Heaven; within which lies the Great Mystery of Kabbalistic Reduction.

<insert>
-> [ LET THE TWO AND THE THREE BE REJECTED, AND THROUGH THE FOUR IT SHALL BE <-
-> REDUCED TO THE SIMPLICITY OF THE MONAD. ] <note>The "Monad" refers to the number one, representing absolute unity or the Divine.</note> <-

This reaches toward the rejection of the binary <note>the number two</note>, just like the border framing the **FIGURE**, in which **UNITY** triumphs over Multitude.
5th. The marks of **NUMBERS** and the characters of **LETTERS**, which refer to the Secret of Mixture; just as
6th. Finally, the **Rosaries** subject to the **WORLDS**, by which many insignia concerning **NATURE, GRACE, and GLORY** are collected into examinations of axioms.
By all these things, the multiform process of both the **DIAGRAM** itself and the **SYNTHETIC GAME** is indicated: which is a certain **METHOD** of discourse concerning any subject **THEME**, making it easy and copious by composing it from the various mixtures of all things which the **FIGURE** provides.
Thus, from these four, as if they were the Hinges of the Universe:

-> **BY DIRECTION, CONSTITUTION, DISTRIBUTION, AND UNIFICATION,** <-
-> **OBJECTIVELY AND SUBJECTIVELY,** <-
-> **GENERALLY, SPECIALLY, AND PARTICULARLY** <-

Once these have been weighed and the vastness of the **UNIVERSALITY** itself has been unfolded (as is right), a not unreasonable institution of **GENERAL DISCIPLINE** seems finally to be fulfilled by that unfolding.
**FURTHERMORE**, because such a mass of **DISCIPLINE** might perhaps not be pleasing to many who are bound by habit to the common yoke of the multitude—or because it might actually be less helpful for the specific conditions of certain people—it was considered whether their studies might be better served if this multitude of disciplines (while preserving the distinctions of each) were reduced to a **Unity of Order**, so that in each one the **DISPOSITION** of the others would be clear. This is likewise being attempted.
And since the **HIGHEST DISCIPLINE** consists in the adequate knowledge of the **OBJECT**, which has already been shown in **GENERAL**...

I have completed the description of the Four Cardinals, which are like the four points of the compass, allegorically designated through the outer limbs of the **CRUCIFIED JESUS**. Furthermore, so that the offspring might more closely resemble the Parent, behold—to one meditating upon these very parts of our **MODEL**, the **CRUCIFIED** <note>original: "CRUCIFIXI"</note> standing before him—there appear opportunely and are indicated by analogy an equal number of proportioned **HEADS** <note>The author uses "Capita" (Heads) to mean the main chapters or guiding principles of each field of study.</note>. By these, the system of **SPECIAL INSTRUCTION** <note>original: "DISCIPLINE SPECIALIS INSTRUCTUS"</note> is filled out by such equal reason that the lower parts correspond to the higher, differing only by their specific classification. For it seemed that the following are signified:

| | | | | |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| | At the Head, | **THE END** | of the Discipline, which [corresponds] to **DIRECTION** | |
| | At the Right Hand, | **NATURE** | of the Object, which [corresponds] to **CONSTITUTION** | |
| **At the Chest,** | | **THE OBJECT** | of the Discipline, which [corresponds] to **UNIVERSALITY** | **Corresponds.** |
| | At the Left Hand, | **DISPOSITION** | of the Object, which [corresponds] to **DISTRIBUTION** | |
| | At the Feet, | **THE SYSTEM** | of the Discipline, which [corresponds] to **UNIFICATION** | |

Therefore, for all the **DISCIPLINES** <note>Fields of study or academic subjects</note> to be brought forth individually according to this very same structural arrangement from that same **MODEL**, that variegated **CIRCULAR ARCH** has been prepared. In this arch, in a single-color and encyclopedic fashion, both the leaders of the **SCIENCES** and the **UNIVERSITY OF THE ARTS** <note>The "University of the Arts" refers to the entire body of human skills and knowledge.</note>, dividing their Objects among themselves, may be seen arranged as follows:

<margin>NAME;</margin>
On the Brow of any **ARCH**, the name is read written in larger characters.
<margin>ICON;</margin>
In the **MIDDLE AREA**, the subject is presented under the image of a certain beautiful **VIRGIN**.
<margin>AUTHOR;</margin>
She is surrounded by two men, one of whom is the Distinguished Author.
<margin>PATRON;</margin>
The other is given by religion alone, as if he were her own in some way—unless it happens that the same person enjoys the title of Author at the same time, in which case he is joined by a certain studious Devotee of that same Discipline, namely one of the **SAINTS** <note>original: "DIVIS"</note> who, either by their profession itself or by some affinity of holiness or action, is seen to have honored the **DISCIPLINE** itself. This is so that no 
<margin>OF THE DISCIPLINES</margin>
single one of our **DISCIPLINES** should lack some sense of piety, which is never out of place for a **CHRISTIAN**.

<margin>PURPOSE;</margin>
<margin>REGARD;</margin>
In the middle, vibrating directly above the rays shimmering above, towers the 
<margin>OBJECT;</margin>
**OBJECT**. In the inner curve of the **ARCH**, beneath the **NAME**, there appears the Formal Definer of the Object, or its specific relation to the **UNIVERSALITY**.

In the middle and larger of the semicircles, which has just come into view: { Nature; Principles; Parts; }
From the semicircles that precede on the right, there comes forth: { First, which is blackish / Second, which is greenish } We find the **OBJECT’S** { Disposition; Properties; Species; }
From the subsequent semicircles on the left, there sprouts: { First, which is yellowish / Second, which is bluish } There come forth the **DISCIPLINE’S** { SPECIES; SYSTEM; INSIGNIA; }

With six rays glowing in a wavy pattern above, various elements—both internal and external—leap forth together with the **ICON OF THE DISCIPLINE**, adorned with its own instruments or symbols. Finally, interspersed among these, in the prominent arches, shields shine forth to complete the whole.

These shields testify that the individual **DISCIPLINES** serve their **SUFFERING LORD** with both the manifold banner of the Saving Cross and selected instruments of His bloody Passion. They distinguish the **THEORETICAL** <note>original: "SPECULATRICES"</note> disciplines from the **OPERATIVE** <note>Practical or applied arts</note> by means of either Eyes or Hands at the summits. At the same time, these parts together sketch out the **FIRST RUDIMENTS OF THE HERALDIC ART**. Walking thus with a **CATHOLIC-CHRISTIAN** pace, that **CHOIR OF DISCIPLINES** seemed to open a way to each individual branch from every side, and to expedite the course of study for each one in no small measure.

Among these, however, **FOUR** sit as the Parents of the others, or as Queens, housed in their own distinguished stations:
{ On the **Right** in the **East**: **METAPHYSICS**; }
{ On the **Left** in the **West**: **MORAL PHILOSOPHY**; }
{ At the **Bottom** in the **North**: **PHYSICS**; }
{ At the **Top** in the **South**: **IDE HOLOSOPHIA**. }
(This last one—his own **IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM** <note>The author coins the term "Ide Holosophia" from the Greek *holos* (whole) and *sophia* (wisdom) to describe a supreme, overarching category of knowledge.</note>—the **AUTHOR** decided to call by joining four words into one. Among the rest, although it embraces them in its bosom as the Parent of all, it is to be placed in a more compact measure.)

Nevertheless, the tireless diligence of the scholar devoted to this task overcame their reason and their stubborn, unteachable nature. Now, each one, crowned with its own equipment, comes forward.

UNIVERSALITY;** | Substance; Actuality; | State, Unions, of the World; | Questions, Laws, | Its Three Faces | Kabbalistic, Hieroglyphic; Mythological, Enigmatic; Proverbial, Parabolic; Alphabetic, Schematic; Pythagorean, Lullian <note>Referencing the combinatorial logic of Ramon Llull</note>; Catholic-Christian, Christian-Kabbalistic. |
| **U. ORATORY;** | Theme, Argument; | Invention, Style, Arrangement <note>These are the classical stages of composing a speech: *inventio*, *elocutio*, and *dispositio*</note>; | Beauty, Energy; | Dogmatic, Emotional. | Didactic, Demonstrative; Deliberative, Judicial; Prosaic, Poetic: Scholarly, Ecclesiastical; Forensic, Military: in the manner of Demosthenes or Cicero. |
| **Y. PRODIGY;** <note>Referring to the "Magic" category from the previous page</note> | Nature, Art; | Active Application, Passive; | Wondrousness, Curiosity; | Of GOD, of Nature, of Art. | Operative, Divinatory; Divine, Diabolical; Physical, Mathematical. |

*These DISCIPLINES are indeed designated appropriately enough by their own IMAGE* <note>original: "IDOLO"; in this context, it refers to a conceptual model or schema</note>
*ordered methodically by each of those which the present [page] reviews in detail*
*having been pursued, it has at last, as it seemed, quite successfully fulfilled and*

*namely, with the Oratory of MAGIC,*
*which HEAVENLY ORATORY established to PRAY for the Saints:*
*HEAVENLY [ORATORY] established to WORK more sublimely.*
*by which one flies toward the inexplicable delights of the SABBATH-REST in GOD,*
*from the things of the UNIVERSE Kabbalistically through CHRIST.*

# 16.
### THE UNFOLDING OF THE MIND FROM THINGS INTO THE SABBATH-REST OF GOD THROUGH CHRIST.

Up to this point, our **GENERAL IDEA of Wisdom** has primarily followed a Universal-Christian <note>original: "CATHOLICO-CHRISTIANA"</note> path; which has proposed that things, viewed as the **UNIVERSITIES enfolded in CHRIST**, should be meditated upon through a sufficiently exact descent from the First things to the Last.

Now, however, it begins to emerge as primarily **CHRISTIAN-KABBALISTIC** <note>This refers to a tradition that integrates Jewish mystical interpretations (Kabbalah) with Christian theology to find hidden divine patterns in the world.</note>; which in turn strives to follow Christ with every duty of worship, by religiously contemplating Him in those same things through a corresponding ascent from the lowest to the highest.

Finally, the mind works to show that one should be wise in every way and in every place—whether in **Reality**, in **Appearance**, or in **Opposition**. In **Reality**, through **NATURE**, whether **HUMAN** <gloss>by which he partakes in every creature; or **DIVINE**, which is inward to all things by its immensity</gloss> and is consistent everywhere. In **APPEARANCE**, it is to be figured through **MYSTICAL** means, such as the Allegorical, Tropological <note>The moral or ethical interpretation of a text</note>, or Anagogical <note>The spiritual or "heavenward" interpretation</note>.
In **OPPOSITION**, however, it is shown through Antithesis and Alienation.

Thus, having begun from the lowest steps of things, it teaches us to contemplate the immediate **CONSTITUTIVE PRINCIPLES OF CHRIST** in the first **PRINCIPLES** of Nature, namely:
*   **MATTER**, showing his Humanity, Subject to DIVINITY;
*   In **FORM**, showing his DIVINITY, Ruling over his Humanity;
*   In **NATURAL UNION**, showing the **HYPOSTATIC** <note>The "Hypostatic Union" is the theological term for the union of Christ's human and divine natures in one single person.</note> Union.
In the **FAMILIES** of Nature, it displays His more excellent **ATTRI-<gloss>BUTES, namely</gloss>**:
*   In **Artifice**, the Wisdom of Divine Art <gloss>wishing to perish</gloss> <note>referring to the concept of "kenosis" or Christ's self-emptying</note> in Him;
<margin>Heav-</margin> *   In **Heaven**, the Breadth of His Charity <gloss>which excludes no one;</gloss>
<margin>enly</margin> *   In the **Element**, the most deserving heads of Moral Virtues in Him;
*   In **Meteors** <note>In the 17th century, "meteors" referred to all atmospheric phenomena, including rain, wind, and lightning.</note>, the Instruments of His twofold Providence;
*   In **Minerals**, the Riches and Ornaments of His House;
*   In **Plants**, the Flowers of His Beauty and the Fruit of His Goodness;
*   In **Animals**, the Sense and Tenderness of His Love;
*   In **Man**, the Sublimity of Reason within Him;
*   In **Angels**, the Sagacity of His Intelligence.
Then, as if Rising again from the lowest state of things, it is elevated to middle things and shows CHRIST

**IN THE FOUNDATIONS OF ETHICS**, namely:

**LAW**, as the PROMISED, PREFIGURED, and EXPECTED one;
**IN THE CHURCH**, as the established HIGH PRIEST and KING;
**IN THE GOSPEL**, as GIVEN and MANIFESTED.
And in different **MORAL STATES**, such as

<column-break/>

Indeed, in the **OPPOSITES** or **DEFECTIVE** aspects of all these, it follows the VERY SAME person, but truly under a much different **APPEARANCE**. For in the **OPPOSITES** of the Principles of Nature, he is shown as a Servant:
*   In the **Vacuum**, by the extinction of His MAJESTY under the **Form** of a servant;
*   In **Privation**, by the Bitterness of Fatherly Abandonment on the Cross;
*   In **Division**, it <gloss>signifies in Death</gloss> the violent **SEPARATION** of His Body and Soul.
In the **OPPOSITES** or **DEFECTS** of the Families of Nature, such as:
*   In **Hindrance**, the Contradictions he sustained against himself;
*   In **Imaginary Spaces**, the immense **ABYSSES** <gloss>or burdens</gloss> of the Sufferer’s Sorrows;
*   In **Corruptive things**, the astounding Deformi-<gloss>ty from wounds</gloss> as if suffering from Leprosy;
*   In the **Monster**, the terrible Figure <gloss>from his wounds</gloss> of the **Sinner** which He took on;
*   In the **Semi-Mineral**, the profound Hardships of His Poverty;
*   In the **Fungus**, His Contempt and Extreme Rejection;
*   In the **Zoophyte** <note>An organism like coral or a sponge that appears to be between a plant and an animal; here used to symbolize a state of transition or lowly silence.</note>, His Silence and the innocent Defects of NATURE;
*   In the **Chimera**, the blasphemous Fictions of those who wander from the truth;
*   In the **Demon** <note>original: "Cacodæmone"</note>, the Power of Darkness and the <gloss>War of</gloss> <gloss>that is,</gloss> Sin.
And in **THEIR OPPOSITES**, namely in all **EVILS**, it proposes His kind Oppositions just as it shows His Hatreds, which are to be feared above all others.
For one may contemplate CHRIST in the **OPPOSITES** of the Foundations of Ethics, such as:
*   In **Unrestraint**, as Sustaining the WICKED until they Repent;
*   In **Heresy**, as Calling back those wandering from ERROR **TO THE CHURCH**;
*   In **Anti-Christianism**, as Enlightening everyone toward Salvation;
In the **OPPOSITES** of Moral States, such as:

Kissing one another in turn: and finally at the height of the Father’s Glory at the Right Hand, and His Rest above every PRINCIPALITY, of whose Kingdom there shall be no End.

Just as here, for the sake of a shorter example, the **IDEAL SHADOW** of our General Wisdom flies, as it were, only over the highest headings of the triple WORLD: so too it can fly through the other parts and points of the FIGURE. Indeed, one may even stand there, in as much as is sufficient, to observe CHRIST everywhere in many ways, where He is also designated. How great, oh GOD! and how innumerable is the harvest of meditations of every kind! Perhaps you, oh vain MAN, excuse the sterility of your soul by a lack of present Material, a penury of Books, or even of Letters—behold, the **VOLUME OF THE UNIVERSE** is now proposed to you wrapped in the **CROSS**. You say it is folded; so be it: but it OPENS itself of its own accord, and No one Closes it, to the one who seeks simply and studiously.
