<meta>This page continues the author’s mystical journey through the "Ideal Shadow," moving from the contemplation of the Sacred Heart to a specific methodology for knowledge, and finally connecting these spiritual truths to the political order of the French Monarchy (symbolized by the Lilies).</meta>

DRAW NEAR then, whoever you are, whether a Student or a Simple person. Draw near, O Man of Desires—draw near, O Disciple of CHRIST, to that Sacred HEART, the Center of the COMPLEX UNIVERSE <note>original: "COMPLICATÆ UNIVERSITATIS"; this suggests a universe where all things are "folded together" or enfolded within the divine</note>. There you shall have a nursery for Infinite Contemplations.

Do you seek an entrance? Lift your eyes to the HEAD, the surest Lighthouse of all DIRECTION. In its light, behold the HANDS, behold the FEET. The RIGHT HAND will lead you into the SOURCES of things and of the SCIENCES; the LEFT HAND will lead you into the STATES, the GENERA, the SPECIES, the INDIVIDUALS of things, and their IMAGES. The FEET themselves will set you within the bonds of the UNIFICATION of things, which is the boundary of the UNIVERSE. With the CRUCIFIED JESUS as your guide, having surveyed all things from the first to the last according to the DIRECTION of the LAWS of WISDOM, retracing your steps and returning to the HIGHEST things, notice everywhere that which the UNIVERSAL whole tells of in many ways: CHRIST. He has been your GUIDE <note>original: "INDEX"</note> through all that you have encountered everywhere on your journey; and He is INDICATED by all things. Indeed, for as many things as there are in the UNIVERSE, there are just as many opportune Oratories for contemplation; just as many LABORATORIES of a higher WISDOM, in which a student is granted both to Pray while studying, and to Study while praying. — In these, indeed, the Faithful Soul, praying in a Christian-Kabbalistic manner <note>The author refers to "Christian Kabbalah," a Renaissance tradition that used Jewish mystical methods to interpret Christian truths</note>, reads the BELOVED in all things through Perception, proves Him through Judgment, and understands Him through Reasoning. In these things, subsequently OPERATING in a Divine-Magical way <note>The term "Magic" here refers to "Natural Magic" or "Theurgy"—the spiritual practice of connecting earthly things to their divine causes</note>, the soul also holds HIM everywhere with Spiritual Sense, loves Him with a Pure Heart, and tastes Him with the Whole Mind. — And as if sublimated upon these Seraphic Wings, the soul flies through HIM into the very bosom of the ETERNAL FATHER. There, at last, far beyond what can be spoken, it delicately keeps its Sabbath rest. — With these things thus conceived, it was desired that a final crowning touch <note>original: "Colophonem"</note> be placed upon the Ideas of GENERAL WISDOM: an ADEQUATE METHOD of EVOLUTION <note>In this context, "Evolution" means the "unfolding" or systematic explanation of a topic</note> for the science itself, which would follow the SUBJECT MATTERS here with every appropriate kind of notion. This was a difficult task indeed, and no less hard to discover than it was necessary. The ORACLE of the CRUCIFIED is consulted in the usual way: and behold, the DIVINITY being CONSULTED through meditation, as in other matters, was present and propitious in this part also. He significantly expressed a FIVE-FOLD SERIES OF FULLEST EVOLUTION through the extremities of HIS BODY, like STELLAR RAYS, as far as it was permitted to be seen, namely:

THE EVOLUTION:
*   In the HEAD, the PROPOSITIVE, which, like the head of the work, would exhibit His sketched Face;
*   In the RIGHT HAND, the POSITIVE, which, by defining individual things, would lay the Foundations of Universal Knowledge;
*   In the LEFT HAND, the EXPOSITIVE, which would assign to each defined thing its own place through Judging;
*   The COMPOSITIVE, which would strengthen judgments with Composite Arguments;
*   And in the FEET, the DISPOSITIVE, which would strive to reasonably apply all things thus organized to their Uses.

Now that you have walked through, MOST BELOVED SPECTATOR, even if only cursorily, the LABYRINTH of our IDEAL SHADOW <note>A likely reference to the work of Giordano Bruno or the idea that earthly knowledge is but a shadow of divine Ideas</note>, and have quickly surveyed—as was permitted by a PRECURSOR—all its curves, hiding places, recesses, and meanders, it is pleasing to finally put an end to the WORK. So that we may send our INTRODUCTION <note>term: "ISAGOGEN" — a Greek-derived word for an introductory treatise</note> toward a festive Epilogue, and more toward the glory of UNITY than for favor, let us conclude with a Problem: Enter the MARGIN again with me. For having begun our prelude from those MIDDLE parts, we will strive for an exit from the CORNERS, which we refer to as LILIES <note>The lily or fleur-de-lis, the royal symbol of France</note>, bringing that admonition from our ONE TEACHER,

->**CONSIDER THE LILIES, HOW THEY GROW.**<-

Warned by this, turn your eyes to the Lilies scattered and shining mixed with Stars around the circuit of the MARGIN. Consider, I pray, those standing on sturdy stalks, breathing something entirely Royal, both in the perfect greenness of their leaves and the splendor of their flowers and their grave habit. From this, conscious of their own Majesty, the FRENCH LILIES <note>original: "FRANCO-GALLICA"</note> come forward of their own accord, by the divine number of their Trinity, and more divine in their bond, which UNITES three under the Indivision of the Stem. Moreover, there is in them a first Root of color and growth: this UNITY of theirs; and it is all the more powerful as it is more rooted in the HOLY MOUNTAIN, WHICH IS THE BOSOM OF THE MOTHER CHURCH, and is subjected to the kind Influence of its own Star, the STAR OF JACOB, namely CHRIST the SAVIOR (whence it is called CHRISTIAN): to Whom it is bound by the constancy of RELIGION and Integrity. To whom, then, might it seem strange that OUR LILIES, so holily undivided, cherished in the bosom of the CHURCH, and most CHRISTIAN under the constant gaze of the CRUCIFIED DIVINITY, should grow so happily? Behold, in WHAT way they themselves answer the one who asks curiously;

->**THE LILIES GROW BY THE VIRTUE OF UNITY.**<-

For from that Source, the Soul of the Kingdom—JUSTICE—is grafted into the LAWS, and the LAWS into the PEOPLE. From that flows the AUTHORITY of Majesty and the STRENGTH of Power. From that, STRENGTH is inspired into Souls, and COURAGE into Strengths, and TERROR into Arms. From that, a Chorus of Good ARTS blossoms. From that, profitable institutions of COMMERCE emerge. And finally, from all these, figured there in its own way, the most flourishing and solid STATE OF THE LILY-BEARING REPUBLIC emerges. 
SEE therefore how OUR LILIES grow by the benefit of such fortunate UNITY, namely under the Special Auspices of CHRIST who UNITES His own things, to Whom they belong most of all, since they are most Christian. Then, under the Patronage of the SINGULARLY ONE MOTHER CHURCH, with her better prayers, as a first-born OFFSPRING she blesses them toward the STABILITY OF UNITY—

<summary>This page describes a mystical methodology for knowledge centered on the body of the Crucified Christ and concludes with a political-theological defense of the French Monarchy, using the "Lilies of France" as a symbol of divine unity and justice.</summary>
<keywords>Sacred Heart, Christian Kabbalah, Five-fold Evolution, Ideal Shadow, Lilies of France, Fleur-de-lis, Unity, Justice, French Monarchy, Christology</keywords>

--- Page 88 ---
<meta>This page continues the author’s mystical and philosophical preface. The catchword "STABI-" at the bottom suggests the next page begins with the word "STABILITATEM" (Stability). The author is transitioning from the contemplation of Christ's physical/mystical body to a formal logical method, and finally to the political symbolism of the French monarchy.</meta>

DRAW NEAR then, whoever you are, whether Studious or Simple. Draw near, O Man of Desires <note>original: "Vir Desideriorum"; a biblical reference to the Prophet Daniel, often used to describe a seeker of divine mysteries</note>. Draw near, O Disciple of CHRIST, to that Sacred HEART, the Center of the ENFOLDED UNIVERSE <note>original: "COMPLICATÆ UNIVERSITATIS"; this implies a universe where all things are "folded together" or contained within the divine source</note>. There you shall find a nursery for Infinite Contemplation.

Do you seek an entrance? Lift your eyes to the HEAD, the surest Lighthouse of all universal DIRECTION. In its light, behold the HANDS, behold the FEET. The RIGHT HAND will lead you into the SOURCES of things and of the SCIENCES; the LEFT HAND will lead you into the STATES, the GENERA, the SPECIES, the INDIVIDUALS of things, and their IMAGES. The FEET themselves will set you within the bonds of the UNIFICATION of things, which is the boundary of the UNIVERSE. With the CRUCIFIED JESUS as your guide <note>original: "Indice"</note>, and having surveyed all things from the first to the last according to the DIRECTION of the LAWS of WISDOM, retrace your steps. Returning to the HIGHEST things, notice CHRIST everywhere in the world, whom the UNIVERSAL whole tells of in many ways. He who was your GUIDE to all things, stay with Him as your Fellow-Traveler <note>original: "Viascum"</note> who meets you everywhere and is INDICATED by all things. Indeed, for as many things as there are in the UNIVERSE, there are just as many opportune Oratories for contemplation; just as many Laboratories of a higher WISDOM, in which a student is granted both to Pray while studying, and to Study while praying. In these, indeed, the Faithful Soul, praying in a Christian-Kabbalistic manner <note>original: "Christiano-Kabbalisticè"; referring to the Renaissance tradition of using Jewish mystical tools to interpret Christian truths</note>, reads the BELOVED in all things through Apprehension, proves Him through Judgment, and comprehends Him through Reasoning. In these places, thereafter, operating in a Divine-Magical manner <note>original: "Divino-Magicè"; here "Magic" refers to the "Great Work" of spiritual transformation and interaction with divine forces</note>, the soul holds HIM everywhere with Spiritual Sense, Loves Him with a Pure Heart, and tastes Him with the Whole Mind. And as if refined on these Seraphic Wings, the soul flies through HIM into the very Bosom of the ETERNAL FATHER: Where at last, further than can be spoken, it Delicately Sabbatizes <note>original: "Sabbathizatur"; to rest in a holy, mystical peace like the Sabbath day</note>.

With these things thus pre-conceived, and wishing to place the Finishing Touch <note>original: "Colophonem"</note> on the general Idea of WISDOM, a proper METHOD OF EVOLUTION <note>original: "EVOLUTIONIS"; the unfolding or "rolling out" of a complex idea into simple parts</note> was required for this science. This method would be flexible enough to pursue every kind of notion across the subjects presented here. This was a difficult task, as hard to discover as it was necessary. The ORACLE OF THE CRUCIFIED was consulted, as is the custom: and behold the COUNSEL. The DIVINE, present and propitious in this part as in others, has clearly expressed a FIVEFOLD SERIES OF THE FULLEST EVOLUTION in the outermost parts of HIS OWN BODY, like STELLAR RAYS, as follows:

### THE EVOLUTION
In the **HEAD**, it is **PROPOSITIVE**, which, like the "head" of a work, shows its sketched face;
In the **RIGHT HAND**, it is **POSITIVE**, which, defining individual things, lays the Foundations of Universal Knowledge;
In the **LEFT HAND**, it is **EXPOSITIVE**, which attributes proper things to everything defined by Judging;
In the **FEET**, it is
{ **COMPOSITIVE**, which strengthens judgments with composed arguments;
AND
**DISPOSITIVE**, which strives to apply all things thus arranged reasonably to their uses.

Now having walked through the LABYRINTH of our IDEAL SHADOW <note>original: "IDEALIS UMBRÆ"; the title of the work, referring to the physical world as a shadow of divine ideas</note>, DEAREST SPECTATOR—though only cursorily—and having quickly surveyed all its folds, hiding places, recesses, and meanders as a FORERUNNER allowed, it is pleasing to finally put an end to the WORK. So that we may conclude our INTRODUCTION <term>Isagoge: a formal introductory treatise</term> with a festive Epilogue—offered less for grace than for the glory of UNITY—enter the MARGIN with me again. For having begun the prelude from its middle parts, we will attempt an exit from its CORNER LILIES, reporting that admonition from our ONE TEACHER:

->"CONSIDER THE LILIES, HOW THEY GROW."<-
<note>A reference to the Gospel of Matthew 6:28</note>

From this warning, turn your eyes to the Lilies scattered among the Stars shining through the circuit of the MARGIN. Consider, I pray, those lilies standing on sturdy stalks; they breathe a Royal air in the perfect greenness of their leaves, the splendor of their flowers, and their serious bearing. From these, conscious of their Majesty, the FRENCH LILIES <note>original: "FRANCO-GALLICA"; the Fleur-de-lis, symbol of the French monarchy</note> come forth of their own accord. They are divine in their number (the Trinity) and more divine in their connection, which UNIFIES three under the indivision of one stem. The first Root of their color and growth is this very UNITY; and it is all the stronger because it is rooted in the Holy Mountain, which is the SITE of our MOTHER CHURCH, and is subject to the kind influence of its star, the STAR OF JACOB—namely CHRIST the SAVIOR (from whence the kingdom is called CHRISTIAN). To this Unity, the kingdom is bound by the constancy of RELIGION and Integrity. Who then should wonder that our LILIES—so sacredly undivided, fostered in the bosom of the CHURCH, and most CHRISTIAN in the constant sight of the CRUCIFIED DIVINITY—grow so happily? Behold, in what way they respond to the one who asks:

->"THE LILIES GROW BY THE VIRTUE OF UNITY."<-

For from that Unity, the Soul of the Kingdom—JUSTICE—is inserted into the LAWS, and the LAWS into the PEOPLE. From there flows the AUTHORITY of Majesty and the STRENGTH of Power. From there STRENGTH is inspired in souls, and SPIRIT is inspired in strength, and TERROR is inspired in arms. From there the Chorus of the good ARTS blossoms; from there the lucrative institutions of COMMERCE. And finally, from all these, the most flourishing and solid STATE OF THE LILY-REPUBLIC <note>referring to the French State</note> emerges. Behold therefore how, by the benefit of such fortunate UNITY, our LILIES Grow, under the special auspices of CHRIST who UNIFIES all things. They belong to Him most of all, since they are "Most Christian." Then, under the patronage of the MOTHER CHURCH, which is uniquely ONE, she favors those lilies with her prayers as an elder OFFSPRING, leading toward the STABI- <note>The text cuts off here, continuing to "stability" on the next page</note>

<meta>This page continues the introductory front matter of the work, transitioning from a dedication or preface into the formal legal and ecclesiastical authorizations required for publication in 17th-century France. The text is framed by a decorative engraved border.</meta>

...and by the Reverend Father Francis Mary of Paris <note>French: François-Marie de Paris</note>, a Preacher of the same Order, we have viewed the light in this way, so that we trust that from it—following the Radial and Spiritual Images—blessings will flow and be supported for the benefit of Students. These students should know that Shadows have a nature pertaining to actual things; and it is not, as Proclus <note>A Neoplatonist philosopher who wrote extensively on the relationship between symbols and reality</note> mentions, that things themselves are affected by shadows, but rather that shadows are moved and grow from the things themselves. Therefore, let this be the **Ideal Shadow**, or the Shadowy Idea of **Divine and Human Wisdom**, offered to the Cultivators of both types of Wisdom. It is fashioned after the pattern of Catholic, Apostolic, and Roman Truth, to which it adheres, and is to be denied to no one who Prays, Labors, and Works—not for the food which perishes, but for that which remains into eternal life. Given at Paris on the Ides of October <note>October 15th</note>, in the Year 1677.

->Approbation of the Theologians of the Order.<-

<margin>
LOISEL, Doctor and Fellow of the Sorbonne, CHANCELLOR of Paris, and Pastor of Saint John in Greve <note>original: *In Graviâ*</note>.
Friar HIEROTHEUS OF PARIS, Professor of Theology.
Friar JEROME OF SENS <note>original: *Senonensis*</note>, Professor of Theology.
</margin>
WE, the undersigned THEOLOGIANS of the Order of Friars Minor Capuchin of Saint Francis, by the mandate of our Reverend Father General, have read and accurately examined a certain Hieroglyphic Work <note>In this context, "hieroglyphic" refers to a work using symbolic images or emblems to convey complex philosophical truths</note> and its Introduction, the title of which is THE IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM, etc. It was authored by the Reverend Father SPIRIT SABBATHIER <note>French: Esprit Sabbathier</note>, then of Bourges <note>original: *Bituricensi*</note>, a preacher of our Order, now deceased. We have found nothing in it contrary to good morals, nor anything foreign to the Truth or the Orthodox and Catholic faith of the Holy Roman Church. Rather, we have judged it to be filled with silent piety and profound learning, organized with the highest genius and order, and therefore worthy to be brought into the light. In testimony of this faith, we have given this statement, signed by our own hands, to the Venerable Father Francis Mary of Paris, a preacher of our Order and the promoter of this same Work since the death of the AUTHOR. Given at Paris on the 9th day of December, in the Year of our Lord 1677.

->Summary of the Royal Privilege.<-

BY PRIVILEGE OF THE MOST CHRISTIAN KING <note>A title for the King of France, at this time Louis XIV</note>, it is granted to Reverend Father FRANCIS MARY of Paris, a Capuchin Preacher, that he may—through any engraver or printer he chooses—commit to the chisel, the type, and the press for a period of ten years the Work entitled THE IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM, etc. This work was devised and composed during his lifetime by Reverend Father SPIRIT SABBATHIER, a preacher of the same Order, along with its introduction. Furthermore, let no one in his entire Dominion care or presume to engrave or print the aforementioned Work, or its Introduction, or any part of it, under any pretext without the consent of the said Reverend Father Francis Mary, or those to whom he has granted his rights regarding this matter, nor dare to offer it for sale. By the same Royal Authority, caution is given under a penalty of three thousand pounds <note>original: *librarum*; referring to French livres</note>, and other threats more fully expressed in the Diploma. Given at Versailles, October 21, 1677.

This Privilege was recorded in the Acts of the Paris Booksellers and Printers on October 7, 1677, by E. COUTEROT, Syndic.

# THE IDEAL
# SHADOW
### OF
## UNIVERSAL

--- Page 102 ---
<meta>This page serves as the formal dedication of the work. It follows the title page and establishes the theological foundation for the author's concept of "Universal Wisdom," framing it as a Christocentric philosophy where Jesus is the beginning and end of all knowledge.</meta>

# TO JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED.

I would believe I did You an injustice, O MY ADORABLE CRUCIFIED GOD, if I intended to begin the IDEA of UNIVERSAL WISDOM more happily, pursue it more surely, and finish it more gloriously THAN BY YOU, UPON WHOM ALL THINGS DEPEND, THROUGH WHOM THEY SUBSIST, and IN WHOM THEY generally RESIDE. It is from YOU, O ALL-powerful GOD, as from the First Cause, that they hold their Activity: it is through YOU, the shining WORD of GOD <note>original: "VERBE," the French equivalent of the Greek "Logos" from the Gospel of John</note>, that they shine to Our eyes: and it is IN YOU, most Pure SPIRIT, that they draw all the strength of their Union. YOU are the Principle of their Unity: the Light, which makes them known; and the Goodness, which makes them lovable. YOU bound their Greatness, measure their Duration, and enclose in YOU the fullness of their Perfection. It is YOU, who drew them from the deep Abysses of Nothingness: and who clothed them with the Beauty of their forms, so as to make them find only in YOU the excellence of their final end. YOU can do all by YOURSELF; YOU know all by YOURSELF; and YOU love nothing outside of YOURSELF; because nothing can be without YOU, there is only chimera beyond YOU, and nothing but horror outside of YOU. It is thus that YOU have regulated the Essence of All Things; that YOU have given them Existence, strengthened their Subsistence, and fixed their Consistence.

But where does it come from, O My Amiable JESUS, that all Creatures thus have their Being from YOU? THROUGH AND IN YOU! if not because it is most certain that YOU are the INFINITE, ABOVE All, being most sufficient of YOURSELF, BY YOURSELF, and in YOURSELF: that is to say, BEING ITSELF by Essence, the Summit of all Perfection; the Source of Essences, the foundation and the Perfection of all things: and consequently Unity, Truth, and Goodness itself. Finally, to say it in a word, being most singularly and most Universally all things. Yes, without doubt, the Beasts will teach it to me: the Birds will learn it from me: Let me ask it of the Earth, although Deaf, she will answer me: the Fish, although Mute, will tell it to me. — And is it not indeed what YOUR Revelation, the Incorruptible Channel of the most sublime Truths rising above senses and human Reason, makes resound much louder than all our reasonings to the ears of those who wish to listen; when She rings out these words through the Mouth of the beloved Disciple: IN THE BEGINNING WAS THE WORD; AND THE WORD WAS IN GOD; AND THE WORD WAS GOD <note>original: "AU COMENCEM! ET ESTOIT LE VERBE; ET LE VERBE ESTOIT EN DIEU; ET LE VERBE ESTOIT DIEU," translating John 1:1</note>: All Things were made by Him, and Nothing was made without Him.

But YOU YOURSELF, LORD, When YOUR servant MOSES asked YOU How and under What Name he should make YOU known to the Rebel People of Egypt; did YOU not openly declare to him by YOUR own Mouth this great MYSTERY, which makes the whole Secret and the Summary of UNIVERSAL WISDOM:!! I AM THAT I AM <note>original: "JE SUIS CELUY, QUI SUIS," the French version of the Divine Name from Exodus 3:14</note>. So that none of Yours could Ignore in the future that this was YOUR NAME, and YOUR TITLE: and if it may be said, that in the Rank of Essences this was YOUR SHARE: which, suiting YOU alone, would also never be communicated to any other. — Is there then now anything FROM WHICH OUR SHADOW could draw a happier beginning than from YOU, who are the substantial Image of the Divinity? By what path could OUR IDEA pass more surely than By YOU, who are the spotless Mirror of the Majesty of GOD? and In WHOM could UNIVERSAL WISDOM finish its course more gloriously than in YOU? Who being the Lord of Sciences enclose all their treasures; and who being King of the Universe, contain so much all things in YOU alone, that it is impossible to see them elsewhere in a more beautiful light.

— And therefore have I not been right to choose YOU Uniquely and to represent YOU on YOUR Cross in the middle of this FIGURE, to dominate the whole Work; so that in relating all things to YOU by a Universal Return, as they were drawn from me by a General Deduction; all this Design may not appear to be anything but YOU, who without YOU would be Insipid and without any taste: It is thus that by the favor of Your Lights we penetrate the Darkness which surrounds YOUR Throne, to perceive there the glory of YOUR Majesty. It is thus that YOUR Day, becoming clearer and more serene, will discover in the obscurities of our <note>The abbreviation "no⁹" typically stands for "nous" (us/our) in 17th-century French manuscripts.</note> [thoughts] THIS ineffable WORD, after which we sigh, hidden in the shadows of a Weak Flesh, and under the Ignominious Darkness of the Cross. Finally, it is thus that YOUR wondrously brilliant Night will make shine in the Darkness of the night <note>original: "la no⁹" here likely refers to "la nuit" (the night)</note> THE TRUE SCIENCE OF SALVATION, which we profess, particularly as preferable to all others.

Receive therefore now as YOURS, O My Adorable SAVIOR, a Work that I desire to be entirely YOURS: and do not permit that it stray in any way either from the sincerity of YOUR Faith, the Unique assured foundation of our knowledge, nor from the rectitude of YOUR Will, the only Incorruptible Rule of all our actions. That is why I submit it sincerely and constantly as a Work which is entirely consecrated to YOU, to the Infallible Judgment of YOUR CATHOLIC and ROMAN CHURCH, the Only True and Without Corruption. ...... Meanwhile I prostrate myself humbly at Your Feet attached to the Cross, O Bloody Spouse of Chosen Souls, to supplicate YOU most urgently that this Crimson Dew of YOUR Blood, which is a Dew of Light and Love, that these Salutary Drops of Your painful Nights, with which not only Your Hair and YOUR Head, but YOUR entire Body are all drenched upon Your Cross, may flow upon our DARK IDEAS: so that being filled with Your Lights, they may shine forth in as many Living sparks of YOUR Knowledge; which, spreading throughout the World, may so happily attract to the Intelligence of THIS IDEA OF WISDOM the Curiosity of Men already quite disposed by the natural desire to know; that the continual Return, which must be made to YOU, O My Amiable CRUCIFIED JESUS, in the reading and study of this Work, may gently and principally insinuate into the Spirit of Unbelievers, preoccupied for a long time, the Salutary Knowledge of YOUR NAME, Inspire more effectively YOUR Love in the Languishing Heart of Christians, and finally kindle everywhere this Sacred fire, which YOU have come to bring upon the Earth, to set the whole World ablaze. Do it then, O MY GOD: I wish it with all my heart: HE IS THE ONLY TRUE GOD, MAY YOUR LORD JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED Bless my Designs, make my Designs fertile, and be if You please, O ABUNDANT GOD, favorable to YOURSELF and to the Glory of YOUR HOLY NAME.

<summary>This page is a formal dedication to Jesus Christ, presenting Him as the source, light, and final end of all universal wisdom. The author blends theology with philosophy, referencing the Gospel of John and the book of Exodus to ground his "Universal Wisdom" in Christian revelation.</summary>
<keywords>Universal Wisdom, Jesus Christ, Dedication, Logos, Word of God, Moses, Divine Name, I AM THAT I AM, Science of Salvation, Catholic Church, Illumination, Mysticism</keywords>

# TO JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED.

I would believe I did You an injustice, O MY ADORABLE CRUCIFIED GOD, if I claimed to begin THE IDEA OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM more happily, pursue it more surely, and finish it more gloriously THAN BY YOU, UPON WHOM ALL THINGS DEPEND, THROUGH WHOM THEY SUBSIST, AND IN WHOM THEY RESIDE generally. It is from YOU, O ALL-powerful GOD, as from the First Cause, that they hold their Activity: it is through YOUR shining WORD <note>original: "VERBE," the French equivalent of the Greek *Logos* from the Gospel of John</note> of GOD, that they shine to our Eyes; and it is IN YOUR most Pure Spirit, that they draw all the strength of their Union. YOU are the Principle of their Unity; the Light, which makes them known; and the Goodness, which makes them lovable. YOU bound their Greatness; measure their Duration and enclose in YOURSELF the fullness of their Perfection. It is YOU who have drawn them from the deep Abysses of Nothingness; and who have clothed them with the Beauty of their forms, so as to make them find only in YOU the excellence of their final end. YOU can do all by YOURSELF; YOU know all by YOURSELF; and YOU love nothing outside of YOURSELF, because nothing can be without YOU, for there is only chimera beyond YOU and nothing but horror outside of YOU. It is thus that YOU have regulated the Essence of all Things; that YOU have given them existence, strengthened their subsistence, and fixed their Consistence.

->But whence comes it, O My Amiable JESUS, that all Creatures thus have their Being from YOU? Through And In YOU! if not because it is most certain that YOU are the INFINITE, above All, being most self-sufficient of YOURSELF, Through YOURSELF, and in YOURSELF; that is to say, BEING itself by Essence, the summit of all Perfection; the Source of Essences, the foundation and the Perfection of all things, and consequently Unity, Truth, and Goodness itself.<-

Finally, to say it in a word, being most uniquely and most Universally all things. If I doubt, surely the Beasts will teach me: the Birds will inform me: let me ask the Earth, though it be Deaf, it will answer me: the Fish, though Silent, will tell me. — And is this not indeed what YOUR Revelation—the Incorruptible Channel of the most sublime Truths rising above the senses and human Reason—causes to resound much louder than all our reasonings in the ears of those who wish to listen; when it makes these words shine forth through the Mouth of the beloved Disciple: IN THE BEGINNING WAS THE WORD; AND THE WORD WAS IN GOD; AND THE WORD WAS GOD. All Things were Made through Him; and Nothing was made without Him. But YOU YOURSELF, LORD, when YOUR servant MOSES asked YOU how and under What Name he should make YOU known to the Rebellious People of Egypt, did YOU not declare to him openly by YOUR own Mouth this great MYSTERY, which constitutes the entire Secret and the Summary of UNIVERSAL WISDOM? I AM WHO I AM. <note>original: "JE SUIS CELUY, QUI SUIS," referencing Exodus 3:14</note> So that not one of YOUR followers could thereafter be ignorant that this was YOUR NAME, and YOUR TITLE: and if it may be said, that in the Rank of Essences this was YOUR PORTION, which, belonging to YOU alone, would also never be communicated to any other.

— Is there then at present anything FROM WHICH OUR SHADOW <note>The author likely refers to his work or human intellect as a "shadow" of divine truth</note> might draw a more fortunate beginning than from YOU, who are the substantial Image of Divinity? Through which OUR IDEA might pass more surely, than Through YOU, who are the stainless Mirror of the Majesty of GOD? and IN WHOM UNIVERSAL WISDOM might finish its course more gloriously than in YOU, Who, being the Lord of Sciences, enclose all their treasures? and who, being King of the Universe, contain all things so completely in YOU alone, that it is Impossible to see them elsewhere in a more beautiful light. — — — And therefore, was I not right to choose YOU Uniquely and to represent YOU on YOUR Cross in the middle of this FIGURE <note>Refers to a diagram or symbolic illustration elsewhere in the manuscript</note> so as to dominate the entire Work: so that by relating all things to YOU through a Universal Return, as they were drawn from YOU by a General Deduction, this entire Design might appear everywhere only as YOU, who without YOU would be Insipid and without any taste? It is thus that by the favor of Your Lights we penetrate the Darkness that surrounds YOUR Throne, to perceive the glory of YOUR Majesty: It is thus that YOUR Day, becoming clearer and more serene, will discover in the obscurities of ours THIS ineffable WORD, for which we sigh, hidden in the shadows of a frail Flesh, and under the Ignominious Darkness of the Cross. Finally, it is thus that YOUR wonderfully brilliant Night will make shine in our Darkness the True SCIENCE OF SALVATION, which we profess, uniquely as preferable to any other.

Receive then now as YOUR own, O My Adorable SAVIOR, a Work that I desire to be entirely YOURS: and do not permit it to deviate in any way either from the sincerity of YOUR Faith, the Unique assured foundation of our knowledge, nor from the rectitude of YOUR Will, the only Incorruptible Rule of all our actions. That is why I submit it sincerely and constantly, as a Work that is entirely consecrated to YOU, to the Infallible Judgment of YOUR CATHOLIC and ROMAN CHURCH; the Only True and Without Corruption... Meanwhile, I prostrate myself humbly at Your Feet attached to the Cross, O Bloody Spouse of Chosen Souls, to supplicate YOU most Instantly that this Vermillion Dew of Your Blood—which is a Dew of Light and Love—that these Salutary Drops of Your painful Nights, with which not only Your Hair and YOUR Head but YOUR entire Body are soaked upon Your Cross, may flow upon our DARK IDEAS: so that being filled with Your Lights, they may shine forth in as many Living sparks of YOUR Knowledge: which, spreading throughout the World, might draw so fortunately to the understanding of This IDEA OF WISDOM the Curiosity of Men, already quite disposed by the natural desire to know; so that the continual Return that must be made to YOU, O My Amiable CRUCIFIED JESUS, in the reading and study of this Work, may insinuate sweetly and principally into the Minds of Infidels, long preoccupied, the Salutary Knowledge of YOUR NAME; Inspire more effectively YOUR Love in the Languishing Hearts of Christians; and finally kindle everywhere this Sacred Fire, which YOU have Come to bring upon the Earth to set the whole World ablaze: do it then, O MY GOD: I wish it with all my Heart: IT IS THE ONLY TRUE GOD YOUR LORD JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED. Bless my Designs, make my Designs fruitful, and be, if You please, O ABANDONED GOD, favorable to YOURSELF and to the Glory of YOUR HOLY NAME.

<summary>A deeply devotional and programmatic dedication framing the "Universal Wisdom" as a Christocentric philosophy, identifying Jesus as the "Word" and "I AM" who centers all knowledge. The author explains that the manuscript's structure and diagrams are designed to lead the reader back to Christ through the "Science of Salvation."</summary>
<keywords>Universal Wisdom, Jesus Christ, Word of God, Logos, Moses, I AM THAT I AM, Science of Salvation, Catholic and Roman Church, Theology, Metaphysics</keywords>

--- Page 106 ---
<meta>This page contains the formal dedication of the work to the Pope, likely Innocent XI. The author establishes a theological continuity between a previous dedication to Christ and this one to the Pontiff, centered on the theme of "Unity."</meta>

## To the Pope.

I DO NOT believe I am multiplying either the Altar or the SACRIFICE, MOST HOLY FATHER, in passing from Heaven to the Vatican to present to YOUR HOLINESS what I have already Offered to JESUS CHRIST. FOR IF CHRISTIAN PIETY cannot suffer Altar against Altar, nor duplicity in the Vows of the faithful: UNITY, which preserves RELIGION EVERYWHERE, does not banish it any less from here. JESUS CHRIST and the CHURCH, of which He has entrusted you with the government, having only one Altar, I also have only one offering to make there: It is therefore the same Present that I bring once more to the same Altar, by offering to you, through a continuous sentiment of RELIGION, what I have already consecrated to JESUS CHRIST. Indeed, if I envisage you as I ought, I cannot but contemplate you in JESUS CHRIST, and contemplate JESUS CHRIST in You through the Union of this alliance, which appropriates to JESUS CHRIST (who is the Base and the Crown of Your Dignity and the source of Apostolic Authority) all the respect one can render to Your Character, and the obedience to Your SACRED Per-son: for it is in HIM that You are Most Holy, Most Great, and sovereign: and HE himself is in You a sovereign Power, an Inexhaustible Virtue, and True Wisdom: and all this, because You are His ONLY VICAR on Earth. As for the rest, what do we see in You more than a Man—but A Man entirely full of JESUS CHRIST <note>original: "tout foü de Jesus-Christ" - literally "mad for" or "consumed by," expressing total devotion.</note>, and His SUBSTITUTE, Who appears to Live only in JESUS CHRIST, and in WHOM JESUS CHRIST alone appears to Live. You live only in JESUS CHRIST through the excellence of that Charity which transforms You entirely into GOD: and JESUS CHRIST alone Lives in You through the expression of His heroic Virtues, which, shining upon the most august Throne, sparkle despite Your modesty in the eyes of all the World. Thus You are no less dedicated to JESUS CHRIST by the Holiness of Your Person, than by that of Your Office: and therefore one must no less respect the merit of INNOCENT <note>A reference to Pope Innocent XI (reigned 1676–1689), known for his personal piety and reforms.</note>, than the quality of SOVEREIGN PONTIFF: and since there is nothing in You that is not of HIM, I estimate that it is equal to honor INNOCENT in JESUS CHRIST, or JESUS CHRIST in INNOCENT. It is therefore to Sacrifice upon the same Altar, and to the same DIVINITY. It is to pursue, and not to repeat, the Sacrifice that I have made to JESUS CHRIST of a WORK entirely dedicated to His glory, to go and present it to Y. H. <note>V. S. stands for "Vostre Sainteté" or Your Holiness.</note> whose Throne it has desired to ap-proach since the moment of its conception. I know not by what secret instinct, if it is not perhaps by that of UNITY: which, reigning powerful-ly in THE CHURCH, draws strongly to its HEAD, as to a secure Rampart, all that seems to fight for its interests. For I am so en-tirely devoted to UNITY, and to the UNITY of UNIVERSAL WISDOM <term>Universal Wisdom: often referred to as Pansophy, a 17th-century intellectual movement seeking to organize all human knowledge into a single system reflective of divine order.</term>; that my whole goal is to reduce the Multitude of SCIENCES, which di-vide This WISDOM into so many pieces, to a SINGLE one; or from all their different Methods to compose only ONE: and to reunite finally (which is the Principal thing) the Minds of Men by the same OBJECTS, which are accustomed to divide them, to a UNITY of intention for that ONE NE-CESSARY thing <note>A biblical allusion to Luke 10:42, "But one thing is necessary."</note>, which makes up the better Part... If therefore the UNITY of UNIVERSAL WISDOM, which constitutes the sole motive of our entire Design, can glorify itself by being allied through several traits of resemblance, whether with THE CHURCH, of which You have the conduct; which, being but ONE, is nonetheless UNIVERSAL; or with THE PONTIFICAL DIGNITY, with which Heaven has clothed You; which is likewise GENERAL, though INDIVIDUAL: or with the Sacred Person of Y. H., Commendable in Heaven and on Earth; Whom an adorable fate has rendered so COMMON and NECESSARY TO THE PUBLIC, though singular in Itself, through the ministry of its Office, that the CHRISTIAN WORLD does not recognize You less for its FATHER, than NATURE makes THE SUN for ITS OWN: since though ALONE, You must however like It be present and provide for All; can it be denied that the Prerogatives of Your Dignity shine singularly in the disposition of THIS WORK; or what Advantages of the latter do not appear admirably in the Excellences of the former? But if it is true, as they say, that Nature is so passionate for things that resemble each other, that She visi-bly assembles those that are far apart by invisible attractions: must one be astonished that the Instinct of the CHURCH, much more judicious than that of Nature, and whose Virtue is much more effective, draws so powerfully to the most sacred Throne This present WORK, as small as it may be in Itself and in its ori-gin, since as much by reason of the UNITY and the UNIVERSALITY of its OBJECT, as by reason of all its others of a manifest resemblance in so many respects, I can say it is allied in JESUS CHRIST to THE CHURCH by an entire conformity of Doctrine: must one, I say, be astonished that it allows itself to be carried away with pleasure by the force of all that it must desire! It is therefore with joy, M. H. F., that This IDEAL SHADOW OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM leaves my hands; or rather that It flies away without fear, trusting in its simplicity to go and satisfy its Vow: that It Goes to present itself at the gates of the Vatican, if It has the good fortune to find them open, to prostrate itself publicly at the feet of Y. H. and receive its Censure, being disposed to any outcome, so that It will be very content, and will hold it for a singular benefit to See itself despised and trodden underfoot, if it is found that It strays from its principal end; which is none other, as I believe I have sufficiently insinuated, than to exactly observe UNITY, and to establish not only the UNITY OF Human WISDOM in Minds; but much more THE UNITY OF CHRISTIAN WISDOM in Hearts: That is to say, THE UNITY of THE SPIRIT in THE CHURCH, THE UNITY of THE FAITH in THE WORLD, and everywhere THE UNITY OF THE RELIGION OF JESUS CHRIST. If, moreover, It has the good fortune to be Judged Useful to this design: It flatters itself already with a much more favorable fate, and dares to hope that under the title of a very humble, very respectful, and very faith-ful service, You will grant to its Innocent desires the grace of THE APOSTOLIC BLESSING, which without dividing its amiable UNI-TY, will make it grow and multiply happily everywhere.

<summary>In this formal dedication to Pope Innocent XI, the author presents his work on "Universal Wisdom," arguing that the intellectual unity of the sciences mirrors the spiritual unity of the Church under the Papacy. He seeks the Pope's blessing for his project of unifying human and Christian wisdom into a single system.</summary>
<keywords>Pope Innocent XI, Vatican, Universal Wisdom, Pansophy, Religious Unity, Apostolic Blessing, Sacrifice, Church Authority, Theological Dedication</keywords>

--- Page 109 ---
<meta>This page serves as the formal dedication of the work to King Louis XIV. The author establishes a mystical and political connection between Jesus Christ, the Pope (Innocent XI), and the French King, presenting his philosophical "Map" or "Method" as a tool for royal study and spiritual devotion.</meta>

## To the King

# SIRE,

YOUR MAJESTY, having had the kindness to question me one day in Bordeaux regarding a report that had reached You—by what happy fate I know not—concerning a then-recent production of my Meditations, which followed a fairly uniform plan despite its multiplicity through a single **MAP**, I remember having the honor of answering You in the local style <note>original: "le style du Pays," referring to a regional idiom or perhaps a humble manner of speaking common in the South of France</note> that it was **A ROYAL METHOD OF STUDY**, so that, when You were moved by that noble Desire, You might go on a **HUNT for the SCIENCES**, pursuing them with pleasure and capturing them without difficulty. **THE QUEEN MOTHER**, of glorious memory <note>Anne of Austria (1601–1666), mother of Louis XIV</note>, who was present, also did me the grace of asking me if there was anything in it for Her. I <unclear>believed</unclear> it right to tell Her that **JESUS CHRIST, THE KING OF KINGS**, who alone could reveal its Mystery to us, was there displayed on the Cross for her Royal Devotion, entirely surrounded by the Heavenly seeds of both forms of Prayer <note>likely referring to the Lord's Prayer and the Hail Mary, or perhaps vocal and mental prayer</note>, which within their small hearts contained a rich harvest of very pious Meditations and Affections, along with very Holy maxims to govern all our actions, and to conform the Course of This Life exactly to the Spirit of His **GOSPEL**. Upon this, YOUR MAJESTY commanded me to produce the Piece immediately; but the Work being still raw and unpolished, I did not believe I could expose it to Your eyes in that state without rashness. That which the lack of light had kept hidden at that time is now, I believe, represented with advantage. **IT IS THIS FIGURE**, which I have dared to title, out of modesty, only **THE IDEAL SHADOW OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM**, more perfect in its form and more extensive in its subject matter; which, beginning to shine through the first rays of its light, comes to satisfy the command of Your Majesty. However, another far more powerful reason engages me to render You this obedience: it is that the vast extent of this **GENERAL FIGURE**, condensed into so little space—and which makes it well known that I am a spirit that comes and goes everywhere in thought—having led me out of the highest of the Heavens, where my Curiosity had carried me to contemplate Jesus Christ at the Right Hand of **GOD HIS FATHER**, led me from there to the Vatican, where I found **THE VICAR OF JESUS CHRIST** <note>The Pope</note> in the Chair of St. PETER, and subsequently conducted me to the summit of France, where I encountered Your Majesty upon the Throne of **THIS KINGDOM**. And by a just comparison of Places and Persons, I found seated in order in each of these so distant Regions all that is most sublime in Dignity upon the highest degrees of honor and Power; namely **JESUS CHRIST** in Heaven, **INNOCENT** <note>Pope Innocent XI, reigned 1676–1689</note> in the **CHURCH**, and **LOUIS** in **FRANCE**. Because, walking in the straight Line of Christianity, I found them All Three so closely united that, although they are three, nevertheless (without offending the Adorable Mystery of that Divine Number) they are but **ONE**. I mean this Unity of spirit, which reunites the Diversity of Nations under a single Christian Name to make them one and the same thing in the perfect Union of the same Catholic Truth. Indeed, is not JESUS CHRIST the One who is yesterday, today, and forever? And who on Earth is more JESUS CHRIST than the unique **VICAR OF JESUS CHRIST**? And who in all the Universe is more **CHRISTIAN** than the **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**? <note>term: "Most Christian King" (Rex Christianissimus) was the hereditary title of the Kings of France</note> They remain therefore **ALL THREE IN ONE: JESUS CHRIST, INNOCENT, and LOUIS**: and the greatest of the three is JESUS CHRIST, since He is **THE HEAD OF THE CHURCH** and the sovereign **KING** of the whole World. It is this happy encounter, SIRE, that gave rise to the favorable opportunity for me to render to Each of them in this Work an authentic mark of my just duties. After having offered my Vows to Jesus Christ and rendered my respects to INNOCENT, His VICAR; is it not reasonable that, in my capacity as Your Subject, I render my homage to Your Majesty? All the more so because, being joined to both—Jesus Christ and His VICAR—even unto the narrow Union of Unity, by those sacred alliances of Divine Religion, Royal sovereignty, and the Holy Spirit which unites them: You participate in all those singular Prerogatives by which **THE CHURCH** embraces all Three indivisibly. For if She is the House of **GOD**, it is You, Sire, who like the Armed Strongman guard the Sanctuary; if She appears as a City, You are its well-fortified Tower, where a thousand shields are found for its defense; if She is considered as a Ship, Your Majesty is a Lighthouse, offering her a safe Harbor in the storm; if one looks at her as a well-ordered Army, one sees in You the **FRENCH MARS**; if She provides the Omen of all Heresies, You are her Christian Hercules; if She fights for Peace, You occupy the rank of **LOUIS THE PEACEFUL**; if one admires her as a flourishing Field, You are its **ROYAL LILY**, the ornament and Majesty of flowers; if one contemplates her as Heaven, You hold the place of Jupiter, of all the Stars the most favorable to Religion; if one holds her as the Mother of the faithful, one recognizes You as **HER ELDEST SON**; if finally this same CHURCH is envisioned as **THE KINGDOM OF JESUS CHRIST**, You fill there, by the grace of GOD, the place of the **MOST CHRISTIAN KING**. It is therefore, SIRE, so as not to stray at all, that I come to render to **LOUIS THE GREAT**—under the expectation of titles of similar duties as to JESUS CHRIST, sovereign Head of THE CHURCH, and as to INNOCENT, its sovereign PONTIFF—and to make to YOUR MAJESTY the same present of the masterpiece of my labor; waiting until, by the help of light from Heaven and the favor of YOUR MAJESTY, I may make the body of the Work more perfect. As its advantageous horoscope seems to promise me by a favorable aspect of Our **FRENCH MARS**; who, having suddenly changed his warlike humor to a peaceful one, to calm **HEAVEN** in favor of **THE EARTH**, and having held back the torrent of his Victories—which the powerful Confederation of all his Enemies had been unable to stop (for there was only a LOUIS THE GREAT who could triumph over LOUIS THE GREAT)—to give Peace to **EUROPE**, makes me hope for a gracious access to YOUR MAJESTY, both for the first part of this Work, which I may call a Work of Peace, having been conceived during the Peace of the Pyrenees <note>A treaty signed in 1659 between France and Spain</note> and brought forth during that of Nijmegen <note>A series of treaties signed in 1678–1679 ending the Franco-Dutch War</note>; and for those parts which are to follow, which shall appear in the Light to the glory of JESUS CHRIST, **FROM WHOM, BY WHOM, and IN WHOM** <note>original: "DE QUI, PAR QUI, et EN QUI," a translation of the Latin "ex ipso et per ipsum et in ipso" from Romans 11:36</note> are generally all Things. Live, SIRE, eternally for this glory, and for the happiness of all **YOUR PEOPLE**.

<summary>A formal dedication to King Louis XIV presenting a philosophical and meditative map of "Universal Wisdom." The author links the King’s temporal power to the spiritual authority of the Pope and Christ, framing his work as a product of the recent European peace treaties.</summary>

# FOREWORD.

<columns>2</columns>

WHY do you pause at the threshold, whoever you are that presents yourself here to contemplate this IMAGE? And why is it that, being on the point of penetrating the Sanctuary and the secrets of WISDOM, you withdraw so quickly in astonishment! Is it perhaps the unexpected encounter with this SHADOW <note>original: "OMBRE"; likely a reference to the title of the work, suggestively referring to earthly representations of divine truths</note> that makes you fear entry? Or perhaps it is this unbelievable alliance appearing in the TITLE—of Darkness with Light and of WISDOM with SHADOWS—that holds your mind in suspense. Notice, if you please, that He who willed to place His Throne in the Sun took pleasure in surrounding it with Darkness; this is why, wishing to provide a sketch of my thought in this MAP, the knowledge of my own lack of Light in such a high undertaking led me to qualify it with the TITLE you see, in order to first revere and highlight the greatness of the subject by a prompt and sincere admission of my INSUFFICIENCY. Perhaps this mixture of Christian Mysteries with those of the Jews under a foreign language <note>The author refers to the use of Hebrew terms and Kabbalistic concepts within a Christian framework.</note> surprises you, and makes you suspect something troublesome? Far from it; it is here that these two Religions hide their holy Alliance, to show us that the secret and Sacred Tradition of the Hebrews, which they call **KABBALAH** <term>Kabbalah: A Jewish mystical and esoteric system of interpreting the Scriptures</term>, agrees perfectly with our Mysteries in the person of JESUS CHRIST. Or perhaps this multiplicity of Figures and Images, this diversity of Colors, and this clutter of Orbs and Circles encased within one another, seem to you an impenetrable LABYRINTH; and fearing the twists and turns, as well as the trouble of extricating yourself, you fear to engage in a place where you perceive neither the entrance nor the exit. But fear not: it is a panic terror. Look at this TITLE, and SHE who supports it: what can one fear under the conduct of WISDOM, and in the company of VIRTUE!

->Wisdom can do all things in its Unity, And if that is not enough:<-
->Virtue well-united triumphs over all.<-

<column-break/>

->For in Wisdom there is but a Single Spirit of Intelligence to know well;<-
->And in Virtue the Spirit of Union is marvelously Effective to act well.<-

Furthermore, consider that you have here the CHURCH represented under the image of WISDOM, and FRANCE under that of VIRTUE. Is it not you, ILLUSTRIOUS FRANCE, who, being the Eldest Daughter of the Sacred SPOUSE of JESUS CHRIST, have chosen from all the Flowers of the Earth ONLY THE LILY for a symbol? But the Holy CHURCH, the sole contemplator of Pure Truth, has taken the POMEGRANATE for her hieroglyph. And as the Unity of the CHURCH HERE WEARS A TRIPLE CROWN, THERE ALSO THE TRIAD OF LILIES IS ADORNED WITH A SINGLE CROWN.

What reason then have you to fear or apprehend, I pray you, under such a powerful protection? Invoke WISDOM, invoke VIRTUE: I mean the WISDOM and the VIRTUE OF GOD, which is JESUS CHRIST. WISDOM will lead you by an admirable Path, and VIRTUE will arm you with the Helmet of Salvation. Call also upon the CHURCH, and reclaim FRANCE: one will defend you with the Sword of the Word, and the other will cover you with the Shield of Justice. Take courage then and animate yourself with Hope, and without borrowing Ariadne's thread <note>Ariadne's thread: In Greek mythology, the tool used to escape the Minotaur's labyrinth; here, the author suggests Christ is a better guide.</note>, do not hesitate to enter with me into this LABYRINTH, under the wise guidance of JESUS CHRIST. He will guide your steps in His paths to prevent you from stumbling; there you will see with admiration Light emerging from Darkness: and you will feel an agreeable hope born within you of arriving more easily by this way, in less time, and much more surely than by any other, AT THE KNOWLEDGE OF THE TRUTH.

And to judge by experience, let us enter, if you please, into this Temple of WISDOM; and after having first made known to you the design of the Work and its occasion, let us lightly run through all its parts with as much exactness as a precursor and an Introduction can allow us, until we can examine each thing in detail in a more ample explanation.

### PREAMBULARY INTRODUCTION TO THE IDEAL SHADOW OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM.

I cannot better inform you, my dear READER, of the occasion I had to invent this new UNIVERSAL SYSTEM, than by the explanation of its Border, and the Mysteries it contains: for there is nothing useless, and everything there speaks a Kabbalistic language; whether to open the Prelude or to close the Epilogue: being thus arranged to satisfy both functions equally. The Prelude divides this Border into the four ordinary parts of the WORLD, which are the EAST, the WEST, the SOUTH, and the NORTH. The top of the Border, as in the SOUTH <note>original: "MIDY"</note> plane of an Inaccessible Light, and under the character of a Luminous **YOD** <term>Yod: The smallest letter of the Hebrew alphabet, often symbolizing the point of creation or the presence of God</term> which is the simplest Letter of the Hebrews and the Origin of the others, which they have judiciously made for this subject a Symbol of the Divine Nature, represents to us Kabbalistically A SINGLE DIVINITY. The Bottom, as in the NORTH <note>original: "SEPTENTRION"</note>, and in its opposite, under the Image of a Venerable Lady pontifically dressed, who offers incense to GOD, proposes to us A SINGLE CHURCH. The Right, as from a clear EAST, under the Figure of a Radiant SUN, discovers to us A SINGLE TRUTH. And the Left, as in its WEST, under the Symbol of a well-polished Mirror, which represents this Sun, to it...

For just as GOD Lives and Reigns in Unity; and as THE CHURCH also carefully preserves the Unity of its Spirit. Thus, now this Illustrious Sequence of Unities, which you see on this Border, [came to me] one day in thought during the profound silence of a pleasant solitude after a serious meditation that I made on the Nature and Perfections of GOD, pausing particularly on His UNITY; for considering Him in the center of His Light, and in the Incomprehensible fullness of His DIVINITY, I saw Him so affect the Inaccessible Solitude of UNITY, that all the different goods that He possesses in Himself cannot please Him without having in them and preserving with the others a perfect Unity: so that He can suffer nothing in THE CHURCH, which is His House, any more than in Himself, if it does not bear the character of this Dignity. Thus, although He is all things by eminence, He is nonetheless so UNIFIED by Nature, that

If He is exempt from change, it is by ONE single **A. IMMUTABILITY**, Which is the absolute Necessity of His Existence;
If He Excels above all, it is by ONE single **B. MAJESTY**, Which is His Incomparable Glory;
If He Reigns over all things, it is by ONE single **C. EMPIRE**, Which is His Sovereign Authority;

(Imagine not, however, that from all these Unities there is formed in Him only a shadow of Multitude: for by means of this Infinity, which embraces them all, His extreme SIMPLICITY unites them so perfectly that of All of them, she makes but ONE.) In the same way, He has chosen for Himself outside of Himself by the decree of His counsel only one single CHURCH, to make Himself known and adored during time and in eternity, and Willed that UNITY should reign over all her Prerogatives, and that She should have

But each of these four things, as the First of its rank, is accompanied by some perfections that are proper to it, and which are remarkable for their Unities; for DIVINITY has its Dignities at its sides; THE CHURCH has its Prerogatives; THE TRUTH, its Advantages; and SCIENCE, its Conditions, all represented by their Emblems, and which in their multiplicity constantly preserve their Unity from its Light.

NOW this Illustrious sequence of Unities, which you see on this Border, occurred to me one day in thought within the profound silence of a pleasant solitude, after a serious meditation I made on the Nature and Perfections of GOD, pausing particularly on His UNITY; for considering Him in the center of His Light, and in the Incomprehensible fullness of His DIVINITY, I saw Him so affect the Inaccessible Solitude of UNITY, that all the different goods which He possesses in Himself cannot please Him without having within them, and preserving with the others, a perfect Unity: so that He can suffer nothing in THE CHURCH, which is His House, any more than in Himself, if it does not bear the character of this Dignity.

To Treat solely, only ONE single **T. SUBJECT**, Which is a Proportioned Object;

To Examine seriously, only ONE single **U. MEANS**, Which is the Exact Definition of the Object;

To Believe sincerely, only ONE single **X. JUDGE**, Which is Right Reason;

Now all these Unities represented by their Symbols having caused me to conceive the Design of a GENERAL SCIENCE to shorten the Time and labor, I have endeavored to form the Idea of it upon JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED, to Sanctify by His Lights and under HIS GUIDANCE BOTH STUDIES AND PERSONS; and to compose from it THIS UNIVERSAL SYSTEM, which under these three different FACES, which THE UNIVERSE has brought to my mind, represents to us:

For just as GOD Lives and Reigns in Unity;
And AS TRUTH makes its own way through Unity;
So this same Truth forms, by its Unity, only one single SCIENCE.

THE UNIVERSE in its CONFUSION is a dark night, by the disorderly mixture of all things contained within it.
IN its COMPLICATION <note>In 17th-century philosophy, "complication" refers to the state where all things are folded together in unity, specifically within God.</note>, it is a rising DAWN, by means of FAITH, which is the first SPARK of our knowledge;
IN its EXPLANATION <note>Meaning "unfolding" or "laying out"; the process of making the complex parts of the world understandable to human reason.</note>, it is a brilliant DAY, by means of REASON which constitutes our entire DAY here below.

<margin>
the night of this Confused UNIVERSE;
the Dawn of this Complicated UNIVERSE;
the Day of this Explained UNIVERSE;
</margin>
THIS black GLOBE situated at the center of the FIGURE Represents...
And the rest of the PARTS of the System
Each according to the ordinary Rules of HERALDRY <note>The system by which coats of arms are designed and described.</note>, by their different Colors, which are marked in the Shield by Hatchings, or different Lines, in this manner, namely:

| Azure (Blue), | Gules (Red), | Vert (Green), | Purpure (Purple), | Sable (Black), | Argent (Silver/White), | Or (Gold). |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| By Horizontal Lines; | By Vertical Lines; | By lines descending to the Left; | By lines descending to the Right; | By Crossed Lines; | By White or Left blank; | By Points (Dots). |

But to properly distinguish all these Colors or Hatchings, one must judge those which are included within the capacity of HEAVEN, in relation to the bottom of THE FIGURE, and look from its Center at all those found outside this circumference.
THE CONFUSED UNIVERSE is in the midst of its night in an inviolable silence to the eyes of those who contemplate it; and presents to them from all sides only the impenetrable abyss of a deep darkness.
THE EXPLAINED UNIVERSE flees into its Day, or at least dazzles the weakness of our Sight by its excessive brilliance. Our Eyes, upon emerging from the shadows of a night, cannot at once endure such great clarity without having become accustomed to it little by little, and having been imperceptibly strengthened by a gentler Light.
But as soon as one comes to envision JESUS CHRIST upon His Cross to invoke Him by this word OPEN <note>original: "APERI"</note>; for it is FROM HIM, THROUGH HIM, and IN HIM <note>A reference to Romans 11:36: "For of him, and through him, and to him, are all things."</note>, that all Things are: It is He who possesses the Treasures of WISDOM: Who holds THE Key of KNOWLEDGE to open and close it at His will, without any other being able to open or close it. Or rather, let us say better, as soon as one comes to consider a little more attentively this COMPLICATED UNIVERSE in JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED, as if in its Dawn; you see it appear upon His CHEST, and radiate around the extremities of His BODY at FOUR CARDINAL POINTS in this order, namely:

<margin>
DIRECTION;
CONSTITUTION;
THE UNIVERSE;
DISTRIBUTION;
ASSEMBLAGE.
</margin>
->HIS HEAD,<-
->HIS RIGHT HAND,<-
**IN HIS CHEST,**
->HIS LEFT HAND,<-
->HIS FEET,<-

<margin>
Just as many ORDERS of Constitutive Reasons;
Just as many CLASSES, or CATEGORIES of Things.
</margin>
THE UNIVERSE appears upon His CHEST, as if in the Center of all COMPLICATION.
DIRECTION is in His HEAD, as if in the Source and the Throne of LIGHTS; for He is Himself the true Light, which enlightens every Man coming into the World.
CONSTITUTION is in His RIGHT HAND;
DISTRIBUTION is in His LEFT HAND;
AND ASSEMBLAGE is in His FEET; which are joined together by a Nail, to represent to us by these ten TOES thus assembled, the ten LIGAMENS <note>Bonding forces or connections.</note> which maintain all Things in UNITY, for the preservation of THE UNIVERSE. It is so that by mentally applying These Four Things upon this ABYSS of darkness to develop them with care, one might dissipate the night, and finally bring forth some Day in this UNIVERSE.
And as the FAITH of JESUS CHRIST (which never counsels anything evil) easily persuades the Christian that in the search for things it is a great happiness for REASON to be preceded by its torch, as by a Divine Dawn, which discovers the Truth to it: It was She also who inspired me to establish upon these FOUR CARDINAL POINTS THIS CATHOLIC-CHRISTIAN MANNER of applying oneself to the knowledge of THE UNIVERSE. and this is what all the Parts of this SYSTEM must serve, which are only to develop these FOUR POINTS according to the idea that I have conceived of them upon the CRUCIFIED BODY of JESUS CHRIST.

THE UNIVERSE in its **CONFUSION** is a dark night, caused by the disorderly mixture of all things contained within it, as if in a **CHAOS**;
IN its **COMPLICATION** <note>In 17th-century philosophy, "complication" refers to the state where all things are folded together in unity, specifically within God.</note>, it is a rising **DAWN**, by means of **FAITH**, which is the first **SPARK** of our knowledge;

-> the night of this Confused **UNIVERSE**; <-
-> the Dawn of this Complicated **UNIVERSE**; <-
-> the Day of this Explained **UNIVERSE**; <-

And the rest of the **PARTS** of the System... Each according to the ordinary Rules of **HERALDRY**, by their different Colors, which are marked in the Shield by Hatchings, or different Lines, in this manner, namely:

| **Azure** (Blue) | **Gules** (Red) | **Vert** (Green) | **Purpure** (Purple) | **Sable** (Black) | **Argent** (Silver) | **Or** (Gold) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| By Horizontal Lines | By Vertical Lines | By Diagonals descending to the Left | By Diagonals descending to the Right | By Crossed Lines | White or Left blank | By Points (Dots) |

But to properly distinguish all these Colors or Hatchings, one must judge those which are included within the capacity of **HEAVEN**, in relation to the bottom of **THE FIGURE**, and look from its Center at all those found outside this circumference.

The **CONFUSED UNIVERSE** is in the midst of its night in a silence inviolable to the eyes of those who contemplate it, presenting them on all sides only with the impenetrable abyss of a profound darkness.

The **EXPLAINED UNIVERSE** vanishes into its Day, or at least dazzles by its excessive brilliance the weakness of our Sight; our Eyes, upon leaving the shadows of a night, cannot at first support such great clarity without having become accustomed to it little by little, and having been insensibly strengthened by a softer Light.

But as soon as one comes to envision **JESUS CHRIST** upon his Cross to invoke him by this word **OPEN** <note>original: "APERI"</note>; for it is **FROM HIM, BY HIM, and IN HIM** <note>A reference to Romans 11:36, "For of him, and through him, and to him, are all things."</note> that all Things exist. It is He who possesses the Treasures of **WISDOM**: Who holds **THE** Key of **KNOWLEDGE** to open and close it at his will, without any other being able to open or close it. Or rather, let us say better: as soon as one comes to consider a little more attentively this **COMPLICATED UNIVERSE** in **JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED**, as if in its Dawn, you see appear upon his **CHEST**, and radiating around the extremities of his **BODY**, **FOUR CARDINAL POINTS** in this order, namely:

-> IN HIS CHEST, THE UNIVERSE; <-
-> HIS HEAD, THE DIRECTION; <-
-> HIS RIGHT HAND, THE CONSTITUTION; <-
-> HIS LEFT HAND, THE DISTRIBUTION; <-
-> HIS FEET, THE ASSEMBLAGE. <-

THE UNIVERSE appears on his **CHEST**, as if in the Center of all **COMPLICATION**.
<margin>As many ORDERS of Constitutive Reasons;</margin>
**DIRECTION**, in his **HEAD**; as if in the Source and the Throne of **LIGHTS**, for He is himself the true Light, which enlightens every Man coming into the World.
**CONSTITUTION**, in his **RIGHT HAND**;
<margin>As many CLASSES, or CATEGORIES of THINGS.</margin>
**DISTRIBUTION**, in his **LEFT HAND**; which marks by its five **FINGERS**—
And **ASSEMBLAGE**, in his **FEET**; which are joined together by a **Nail** <note>The OCR reads "Cloud," likely a misreading of the French "Clou" (nail).</note>, to represent to us by their ten **TOES** thus assembled, the ten **LIGAMENTS** which maintain all Things in **UNITY**, for the preservation of the **UNIVERSE**. It is so that by mentally applying these Four Things upon this **ABYSS** of darkness to develop them with care, one may succeed in dissipating the night, and finally bring forth some Day in this **UNIVERSE**.

And as the **FAITH** of **JESUS CHRIST** (which never counsels anything evil) easily persuades the Christian that in the search for things it is a great happiness to have **REASON** preceded by its torch, as by a Divine **DAWN** which discovers the Truth to him; it was She also who inspired me to establish upon these **FOUR CARDINAL POINTS** this **CATHOLIC-CHRISTIAN MANNER** of applying knowledge to the **UNIVERSE**. This is what all the Parts of this **SYSTEM** must serve, which are only for developing these **FOUR POINTS** according to the idea I conceived of them upon the **CRUCIFIED BODY** of **JESUS CHRIST**.

### II. THE CONSTITUTION OF THE UNIVERSE
Established by These Five ORDERS OF REASONS, Which are
- THE HYPOSTATIC <note>Relating to the fundamental substance or underlying reality of a thing.</note>,

- THE STATIVE <note>The principles relating to stability or a fixed state.</note>,

<margin><image-desc>A geometric symbol consisting of a triangle atop a vertical stem, with three dots or small circles at the vertices.</image-desc></margin>
Simplicity. It sends forth three Rays of Life, one of which goes to the right and the other to the left, representing THE VIVIFYING VIRTUE of the Zodiac. The rest, imitating the Sun, and the Third ray, which is straight in the middle, reuniting the Virtue of the other two at its sides, forms from the Multiplicative Virtue of the <term>Archaeus</term> <note>The vital life-force or "master-builder" in the body, a term common in Paracelsian alchemy.</note> the Seminary of Nature to Vivify the Flesh of the Terrestrial Body by the Virtue of this word: <note>original: "FIAT"</note> "Let it be done"; and of this Commandment: <note>original: "CROISSEZ, et MULTIPLIEZ"</note> "INCREASE and MULTIPLY." Finally, it is this Mystic SILT <note>The "limon mystique" refers to the clay or dust of the ground from which Adam was formed.</note>, from which the Animal Man was formed by the Hand of GOD, as evidenced by this Sign TAV or Δ [Kabbalistic Aleph, otherwise THE SACRED TERNARY, THE ARCHETYPE of the Immortal Soul which Man wore marked on his forehead before his fall, entire and without division, and united in the Mysterious <term>Trisagion</term> <note>From the Greek for "Thrice Holy," referring to the angelic hymn "Holy, Holy, Holy."</note> of DIVINE UNITY. This rendered him Immortal, but it was broken by his fall, and thereafter remained open; which is why, floating ever since in the Instability of the Binary of Matter, he experiences every day the discord and oppositions of the Quaternary into which he has fallen. From this come Sickness, Corruption, and Death: but the Incarnation of JESUS CHRIST has repaired this loss through the Sacrament of Regeneration; just as His Passion has through the Glory of the Resurrection.

<margin><image-desc>A circular symbol containing a cross with four dots in the quadrants.</image-desc></margin>
IT IS the Perfection of the UNIVERSE in the Mystic Work of the six Days; where the World is assigned a High and a Low; an East and a West; a South and a North. Thus, this Hieroglyph of the World reveals the seven Lights in the Mystery of the seven Days of Creation: for the Center of the Senary <note>The number six.</note> makes the <term>Septenary</term> <note>The number seven, considered a number of rest and completion.</note>, upon which NATURE rolls and rests; and which GOD chose to Sanctify His Adorable NAME. I say, therefore, that THE LIGHT of the World comes from the Septenary, because one ascends from it to the <term>Denary</term> <note>The number ten, signifying perfection or the return to unity.</note>, which is the Horizon of Eternity, from which all power and the Virtue of things proceed.

<margin><image-desc>A small circle with a central dot.</image-desc></margin>
IT IS THE VIRTUE of the Prime Mobile of the World, or The Astral and continual Movement of NATURE, which imitates the course and perfection of the Circle in the perpetual Economy of generations; for it is the Heavens that, from the fertile Treasures of Seminal Reasons, draw out the Regeneration of Individuals: so that by means of the great Ascendant of the MONARCHY of the WORLD, things may imitate the Eternity from which they emerged, through the Firmament of their seeds.

IT IS THE UNIVERSAL SPIRIT of the WORLD, capable of producing all things, which by means of the <term>Azoth</term> <note>In alchemy, the universal solvent or soul of the world, containing the first and last letters of the alphabet (A and Z) to signify wholeness.</note>, or the Soul of the World, spreads the Virtue of Seeds in every direction, and insinuates Life into everything to maintain the Harmony of the World's Economy. It is this Spirit that renews and regenerates all things, and which, pushing seminal Virtues from the Circumference to the Center of the World, and bringing them back from the Center to the Circumference and onto the face of Matter, joins Inferior things to Superior ones, and Celestial things to Terrestrial ones. Thus, Generation is nothing other than the descent of the Azoth, or the Flow of Seeds.

IT IS THE Individual, exterior, and perfect STATE of the Existence of each Body, remaining fixed in its Being and upon the Cube of its own solidity, consisting in the temperament of the four Vital qualities of the ELEMENTS, which nourish, grow, and preserve it in its corporeal being. These are, I say, the four Mothers of Natural things—that is to say, THE ELEMENTS—from which all Mixed bodies emerge and to which they generally return. Thus, the Death and Corruption of Natural things is nothing other than the Return of the AZOTH, or the Reflux of Seeds.

Below all these figures, this Sphere of the Divine World similarly prepares its ALPHABET, its THEOLOGICAL ROSARY, applied singularly to GLORY in this manner:

| **BC** | in the multitude of created goods. | **KL** | Glory, which must then last as long as Eternity. | **St** | necessary for such a noble end. |

<meta>This page continues the author’s explanation of his "General Science," specifically detailing how he intends to organize every specific field of study (Particular Sciences) into a unified visual and conceptual system based on the anatomy of the Crucifix.</meta>

<image-desc>A small decorative cross is placed above the first line of text.</image-desc>

One will not, in fact, be able to accommodate oneself. I have examined whether it would not be more advantageous for the studies of many people—even to shorten their time and effort—to reduce this great Number of Sciences to the unity of a single Order, without however confusing their particular differences, so that one could see in each one THE ARRANGEMENT of All the Others; this is what I have STRIVED to do once again. AND as THE CAPITAL <note>In this context, "capital" refers to the core or head of the matter.</note> of a SCIENCE consists of the perfect Knowledge of its OBJECT; as one was able to notice in GENERAL SCIENCE through the development of these Four Cardinal Points, signified allegorically by the Four Extremities of the Crucified Body of JESUS CHRIST: when I was working one day on this idea, striving to make the Daughter <note>The "Daughter" refers to a specific, individual branch of study (a Particular Science), while the "Mother" refers to General Science or universal knowledge.</note> more like the Mother; and while I was contemplating OUR ordinary MODEL, which was before my eyes, it seemed to me that the parts OF THE CRUCIFIX marked for me very appropriately, and by a quite precise relationship, as many CAPITAL POINTS which could compose THE SYSTEM of PARTICULAR SCIENCE in an order so equal and so uniform, that all the Inferior and Particular things would correspond perfectly to the Superior and General ones, and would differ from them only by their specific details: for it seemed to me that one could very well represent:

<summary>The author presents a mystical and visual framework for organizing all knowledge, using the proportions of the crucified Christ as a structural guide. He describes a complex diagram involving arches, colors, and heraldic symbols to represent the components of each science, such as its goals, principles, and patrons.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the introduction to a monumental "Catholic-Christian Encyclopedia," focusing on the organizational structure of the sciences as envisioned by the author. It transitions from the general purpose of the work to a specific, quasi-spatial diagram of knowledge.</meta>

It is upon this foundation that **THIS CATHOLIC-CHRISTIAN ENCYCLOPEDIA** must proceed, in order to open an easy path to **EVERY SCIENCE** from all sides, and to significantly shorten their difficulties and their course of study.

But to avoid the confusion that might arise from their great number, I have placed **FOUR** of them more honorably in the principal positions, because they are like the Mothers and the Queens of the others. Thus, you see that the author, uniting four words into one, has seen fit to name his **IDEAL SHADOW OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM** <note>original: "OMBRE IDEALE DE LA SAGESSE UNIVERSELLE." This title suggests a Neo-Platonic or Hermetic influence, where earthly knowledge is a "shadow" of divine truth.</note>, having wished to reduce it to a small scale to place it in the center. Furthermore, each of these four has its most illustrious branches at its side. And so:

<image-desc>A hierarchical classification diagram using organizational brackets to categorize sciences by cardinal directions and primary subjects. The text is structured into groups linking directions (Orient, Occident, Septentrion, Midy) to the four main sciences and their sub-disciplines.</image-desc>

**METAPHYSICS**
**MORALS** <note>In this context, "Morals" or "Ethics" refers to the study of human conduct and virtue.</note> are ranked with the others, although as their MOTHER, and in her capacity as UNIVERSAL, she encloses them all within her breast.
**PHYSICS** <note>In the early modern period, "Physics" referred to the study of all natural phenomena, not just matter and motion.</note> has fourteen branches, which are:
**IDEO-HOLOSOPHY** <note>original: "L'IDEHOLOSOPHISCIE." A coined term likely combining "Idea," "Holo" (whole), and "Sophia" (wisdom), representing a holistic science of ideas.</note> regards them all as her daughters, because they all emerge and descend from her on every side. But the closest ones, which are at her sides as her most noble instruments, are:

*   To the Right, in the East — **METAPHYSICS;**
*   To the Left, in the West — **MORALS;**
*   At the Bottom, in the North — **PHYSICS;**
*   At the Top, in the South — **IDEO-HOLOSOPHY.**

(It is thus:
*   To its Left it has Pneumatics <note>The study of spiritual beings, souls, or "breath."</note> and Theology;
*   To its Right it has Jurisprudence <note>The philosophy and science of law.</note> and Politics:
*   The Seven Liberal Arts <note>The traditional curriculum of Grammar, Logic, Rhetoric, Arithmetic, Geometry, Music, and Astronomy (here replaced by Cosmography and Perspective).</note> are to its right, namely Grammar, Logic, Arithmetic, Geometry, Cosmography, Music, and Perspective;
*   The Seven Mechanical Arts <note>Practical or manual crafts, often contrasted with the "liberal" or intellectual arts.</note> are to its left, namely Agriculture <note>original: "Georgique."</note>, Weaving, Architecture, Hunting, Military Science, Navigation, and Medicine.
*   To the Left: **RHETORIC**,
*   To the Right: **MAGIC** <note>In this era, "Magic" often referred to "Natural Magic"—the study of the hidden forces of nature, similar to early experimental science.</note>.
