But while I was trying to arrange all those subjects I have just reported under this same order, I perceived that most of them were so difficult to manage, and of such a different and troublesome nature, that I saw no hope that one could ever harmonize such diverse and stubborn tempers under a single rule, nor that they could **ALL** be reduced to a single **FORM of METHOD**. However, a more serious reflection has corrected their great eccentricity, and their almost insurmountable resistance has allowed itself to be conquered by the diligence of more stubborn work. Thus, having now been tamed and subjected to a very equal and uniform **ORDER**, you see them appear here, **EACH** with its entire **SYSTEM** in an equal and similar arrangement, in this manner:

The entire SYSTEM of each of these SCIENCES is represented quite appropriately by its IMAGE: but it is only the Methodical ASSEMBLY and the Indissoluble UNION of all THE PARTS reported in detail in THIS TABLE <insert>Composed</insert>

Which our GENERAL SCIENCE, having begun under favorable auspices and pursued with some success, seems to have completed with sufficient happiness: as she explains here, forming by herself, along with her two companions, Rhetoric and Magic, the joyful conclusion of this entire CHOIR OF THE SCIENCES.
I mean to speak principally of Christian Rhetoric and DIVINE Magic; both of which come from Heaven to teach us: ONE, to PRAY more holily; the OTHER, to OPERATE <note>To act or perform works</note> more nobly.
It is upon these two wings of a Seraph <note>A high-ranking angel often depicted with six wings.</note> that from all things in the UNIVERSE, one rises Kabbalistically THROUGH JESUS CHRIST even unto the Inexplicable Delights of GOD HIMSELF; and that one happily flies into His Bosom to rest there. This is the summary and the SUMMIT OF PERFECTION.

--- Page 171 ---
<meta>This page continues the author’s "Universal Wisdom" project. Having previously explored how Christ is contained within the universe (a descending view), the text now shifts to an ascending view—how the physical and moral world reflects Christ’s nature, both in its perfection and in its suffering (opposites).</meta>

# THE FLIGHT OF THE SPIRIT TO RISE ABOVE CREATURES AND GO THROUGH JESUS CHRIST TO REST IN GOD.

The idea of our Universal Wisdom has appeared until now as **CATHOLICALLY-CHRISTIAN**, in that it descended quite precisely from the First Parts of the **UNIVERSE** down to the Last, considering them all as enfolded <note>original: "compliquées"; in this philosophical context, it suggests that all things are folded into or contained within a higher unity.</note> in **JESUS CHRIST**. It will now appear as **CHRISTIANLY-KABBALISTIC** <note>The author uses "Kabbalistic" here to refer to a mystical, symbolic interpretation of reality common in 17th-century esoteric Christian thought.</note>; and in rising from the Lowest to the Highest, it contemplates **JESUS CHRIST** with respect in his turn, honoring him as represented by his Virtues in all these same things.

After having led us to discover through **FAITH**, to know through Reason, and to penetrate through Understanding the adorable Presence of this **GOD-MAN**; this wisdom strives to represent him to us either in his Reality, or in his Image, or in his Opposition, so that we may taste him in Spirit in all things, and in every manner.

It represents him to us in his Reality by his own **NATURE**: either by his **HUMANITY**, which allows him to communicate with all Creatures, or by his **DIVINITY**, which renders him Intimately present to all things and in all places by his Immensity.

In his Image, it represents him to us mystically, either by some Allegory, or by some Morality, or by some Anagogy <note>Anagogy is a method of spiritual interpretation that finds a hidden "uplifting" or heavenly meaning in words or events.</note>.

Finally, in his Opposition, it represents him to us either under some Antithesis, or some Disproportion.

Thus, to begin with the Lowest Level of the **UNIVERSE**, it teaches us to contemplate in the First Principles of Nature those **PRINCIPLES** which immediately establish **JESUS CHRIST** in his Being; namely:

->IN MATTER, his Humanity subject to his **DIVINITY**;<-
->IN FORM, his **DIVINITY**, which presides over his Humanity;<-
->THE NATURAL UNION, or the **HYPOSTATIC UNION** <term>Hypostatic Union: The theological doctrine that the divine and human natures are united in the single person of Jesus Christ.</term>.<-

IN THE FAMILIES of Nature, it reveals to us his most beautiful **ATTRIBUTES**; namely:

| In | Source | The Attribute Revealed |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| | **Artifice** | The Wisdom of his entirely divine Art; |
| | **The Heavens** | The extent of his Charity, which wills that none should perish; |
| | **The Elements** | The most excellent of his Moral Virtues; |
| | **The Meteors** | The Organs of his double Providence; |
| | **The Minerals** | The Riches and Ornaments of his House; |
| | **The Plants** | The Flower of his Beauty, and the Fruits of his Goodness; |
| | **The Animals** | The tenderness and Sensibility of his Love; |
| | **Men** | The Sublimity of his Reason; |
| | **Angels** | The Subtlety of his Intelligence. |

Then, rising from the Lowest of things to their Middle Region, it shows us **JESUS CHRIST**
IN THE FOUNDATIONS of Morals,
Such as
IN THE LAW: PROMISED, PREFIGURED, and AWAITED;
IN THE CHURCH: Established by **GOD** as PRIEST and KING;
IN THE GOSPEL: GIVEN and MANIFESTED.
In the various STATES of Morals, it shows us his **PERFECTIONS**; such as:
In Innocence, his Impeccability;
In Liberty, the Rectitude and Integrity of his Free Will;

<column-break/>

BUT in the OPPOSITES, or the DEFECTS of all this, it gives us a very different Image; for it reveals to us in the OPPOSITES of the Principles of Nature:
As **The Void**, the Annihilation <note>An allusion to 'kenosis' or the self-emptying of Christ.</note> of his **MAJESTY** under the form of a Servant;
As **Privation**, the Inconceivable Rigor of the abandonment by his **FATHER** on the Cross;
As **Division**, the violent Separation of his Soul from his Body through Death.

And in the OPPOSITES, or the DEFECTS of the Families of Nature:
As **The Obstacle**, the Contradictions he suffered from Sinners;
As **Imaginary Space**, the Impenetrable Abyss of his sorrows in his **PASSION**;
As **The Corruptive**, the terrifying Ugliness—similar to that of a Leper—caused by his Wounds, in which he was clothed;
As **The Monster**, the horrible Figure of the Sinner, which he took upon himself;
As **The Semi-Mineral**, the extremities of his most high Poverty;
As **The Mushroom**, his Extreme Contempt and profound Lowliness;
As **The Zoophytes** <term>Zoophytes: Organisms like coral or sea-anemones that look like plants but are animals; used here to symbolize a state between life and stillness.</term>, his admirable Silence, and the Innocent Defects of his **HUMANITY**;
As **The Chimera**, the Blasphemies of the Impious, and the delusions of Unbelievers regarding him;
As **The Demon**, the Power of Darkness, and the War of Sin.

IN THE OPPOSITES of these things, which are ALL THE EVILS of MORALITY against him, it proposes to us his extreme GENTLENESS in bearing our defects, and the sad effects of his just Wrath, and his formidable Hatred: for it shows him to us IN THE OPPOSITES of the foundations of **MORALS**, as Licentiousness Awaiting the **WICKED** for Repentance;
IN HERESY, Calling those who have gone astray back to the bosom of the **CHURCH**;
In Anti-Christianity, Enlightening all Men for Salvation.

And in the OPPOSITES of the States of Morals, such as:
In **Sin**, Merciful in Forgiving;
In **Slavery**, Inviting to Rest those who are Burdened and weary;

Do not be astonished now if the IDEAL SHADOW OF OUR Universal WISDOM passes so lightly over all these matters: it is so as not to weary you by the length of the examples; she only gently touches upon the Principal POINTS of these Three WORLDS, in order to leave you the pleasure and the liberty of enjoying yourself everywhere where you yourself may briefly see the other PARTS OF THE FIGURE <note>the author refers here to a visual diagram or "Figure" that accompanied this text</note>, and to stop there as long as you please; to Remark JESUS CHRIST everywhere, and in all the manners that are explained there. Judge what a prodigious and abundant house of Meditations of all kinds.

You will perhaps excuse yourself, but in Vain, on the sterility of Your spirit; on the lack of present matter; and on the lack of Books, or studies: for HERE is the Great Book of THE UNIVERSE, which is presented to you on the Cross. You complain that it is closed; it is True: but it opens easily of Itself, without anyone being able to close it, to Him who seeks to read it with care, and in great simplicity of heart.

Draw near then, whoever You May Be, with Love and sincerity: draw near with a heart full of desires. Come with the quality of a Disciple of JESUS CHRIST, and draw near to this Sacred BREAST, which makes the Center of THE COMPLICATED UNIVERSE. You will find there a nursery of Infinite contemplation.

You seek perhaps where to enter: raise your eyes to this August HEAD, it is there the assured Lighthouse of all our CONDUCT. Take

<meta>This page continues the introductory explanation of a mystical and philosophical system, likely from the 17th century, which uses the physical form of the crucified Christ as a mnemonic and structural map for universal knowledge. The author transitions from describing the spiritual contemplation of Christ's body to defining the "Complete Method of Science" that will govern the rest of the book.</meta>

This Light allows one to consider both His HANDS and His FEET. His RIGHT will lead you to the SOURCES of THINGS and of the Sciences: His LEFT will lead you through all their STATES, their GENRES, their SPECIES, their INDIVIDUALS, and their IMAGES: His FEET will make you see THE LINKS, which make up THE ASSEMBLY and THE UNION of things: where the entire UNIVERSE tends and concludes.

BUT after you have considered all these things from the First to the last following the LAWS and Maxims of WISDOM under the favorable Guidance of JESUS-CHRIST-CRUCIFIED: retrace your steps, and ascending even to the HIGHEST, notice JESUS-CHRIST everywhere, whom all these things make known to us in so many ways; and admire to see that He, who discovered all things for you, meets Himself everywhere, and is reciprocally discovered by all things—from which one must conclude that as many things as there are in the UNIVERSE, they are so many ORATORIES fit for Contemplation, and so many Laboratories of a most high WISDOM, where one can STUDY while praying, and PRAY while studying.

It is there that in a Christian-Kabbalistic <note>Christian Kabbalah was a Renaissance and Early Modern movement that integrated Jewish mystical traditions with Christian theology to find hidden meanings in scripture and nature.</note> Prayer a Faithful Soul perceives her BELOVED in everything through Thought: finds herself there through Judgment; and Comprehends Him there through Reasoning.

It is there that afterwards, by a Divinely-Magical Operation, she embraces Him in everything through a Spiritual Feeling: Cherishes Him with a Purified Heart; and tastes Him with all her Spirit: and that rising upon these two Seraphic Wings, she flies by means of her BELOVED into the bosom of the ETERNAL FATHER to enjoy there a Delicious Rest, which surpasses all that one can say or think.

All these things being thus well conceived and disentangled, nothing more was lacking to Crown Our Idea of UNIVERSAL WISDOM than to set out upon the Plan of its EXPLANATION THE COMPLETE METHOD OF SCIENCE to treat with order, and explain in all suitable ways THE OBJECTS OF OUR KNOWLEDGE, which are contained in this WORK. This was a matter of great study, and as difficult as it was necessary to find.

For this, I had recourse, according to my custom, to OUR CRUCIFIED ORACLE: and after having consulted His DIVINITY through prayer and meditation, HE, making Himself present and favorable in this as in all the rest, appeared to me to divide very pertinently through the Extremities of His extended BODY—as by so many RADIANT STARS—THE ENTIRE and PERFECT EXPLANATION of SCIENCE into FIVE PARTS, and to mark very expressly:

BY
{ HIS HEAD, THE PROPOSITIVE; Which, forming the Head of the work, revealed its Face and Design;
{ HIS RIGHT [HAND], THE POSITIVE; Which, by Defining each thing, laid the Foundations of all our Knowledge;
{ HIS LEFT [HAND], THE EXPOSITIVE; Which Judged defined things by Attributing to them what Suits them;
{ HIS FEET,
    { THE COMPOSITIVE; Which Supported its Judgments by Reasonings, which she composes;
    { And
    { THE DISPOSITIVE; Which, having thus well Digested all things, worked to arrange them in good order, and to apply them
                                                                    judiciously to their own Uses.

It is time now, MY DEAR READER, after having traversed, though quite promptly, THE LABYRINTH of Our IDEAL SHADOW <note>The "Ideal Shadow" likely refers to the symbolic or diagrammatic representation of the divine order described in the preface.</note>; after having Visited, though in haste and as if in passing, all the twists and turns, the nooks and crannies, as much as is permitted to a FORERUNNER: it is, I say, time to finish the course; and to close OUR INTRODUCTION with some pleasant Problem in favor and to the glory of UNITY, let us return, I pray you, through this BORDER: and since we have entered into the explanation of THE WORK, and have begun the Prelude with these Four CARTOUCHES <note>A cartouche is an ornamental frame or scroll-like decoration often found in maps or architectural drawings of this period.</note>, which form the MIDDLE of the Four SIDES OF THE BORDER: let us exit by the CORNERS of this same BORDER, and finish the Epilogue with the Four FLEURS-DE-LYS, upon which you see Engraved this Advice from our ONLY MASTER: CONSIDER THE LILIES, HOW THEY GROW. <note>original: "CONSIDEREZ LES LYS, COMMENT ILS CROISSENT." A reference to Matthew 6:28.</note>

Indeed, cast your eyes upon the brilliance of these Lilies scattered here and there, mingled with these Stars all along this BORDER: consider them, I pray you; and see in the gravity of their bearing nothing but Royalty, whether by the height and Solidity of their stem, or by the freshness and Greenness of their Leaves, or finally by the whiteness and Majesty of their Flowers; so that, feeling their Nobility, they Willingly declare themselves entirely FRENCH; being already completely DIVINE by the Triad of their number, and much more by their close union, which assembles Three upon a single trunk.

Do you know what the Radical Principle of this admirable whiteness and the growth of our LILIES is? It is their singular UNITY: which is all the stronger as they are more deeply rooted on the Holy Mountain, which is Our MOTHER, THE HOLY CHURCH, and as they are better exposed to the benign Influences of her STAR, THE STAR OF JACOB <note>The "Star of Jacob" is a messianic reference from Numbers 24:17, traditionally identified by Christians as Jesus.</note>, OUR SAVIOR JESUS CHRIST, from whom they borrow the name of CHRISTIANS, and in whom they take glory in being Irrevocably subject through the Constancy and Integrity OF RELIGION. Is it then any wonder after this that OUR LILIES, so Holily Invisible, Raised in the Bosom of the CHURCH, and made MOST CHRISTIAN <note>The author is playing on the title "Most Christian King" (Roi Très-Chrétien) traditionally held by the Kings of France, symbolized by the Fleur-de-lis.</note> by the fixed and continual gaze of a CRUCIFIED GOD, Grow so happily. Behold, they themselves answer "How" to all Those who are Curious to know.

<meta>This page continues the allegorical and political praise of the French Monarchy and the Papacy, focusing on the theme of Unity as the foundation of power and order. It likely follows a page establishing the divine origins of royal symbols.</meta>

# THE LILIES GROW BY THE VIRTUE OF UNITY.

For it is by means of **UNITY** that **JUSTICE**, which makes the **SOUL** of a **KINGDOM**, instills itself into its **LAWS**, and its **LAWS** into its **PEOPLE**. It is she <note>Unity, personified here as a feminine principle in French.</note> who creates the **AUTHORITY** of **Royal Majesty**, and the **SINEW** of its **Power**. It is she who gives **STRENGTH to HEARTS**, **COURAGE** to its **STRENGTH**, and **TERROR** to its **Victorious Arms**. Who makes **THE ARTS** succeed, **THE SCIENCES flourish**, and **COMMERCE prosper**. It is she, finally, who through all these things forms and maintains in its splendor the flourishing **EMPIRE OF THE REPUBLIC OF THE LILIES** <note>In this context, "Republic" refers to the "Commonwealth" or the State of France, symbolized by the fleur-de-lis.</note>. This is how **OUR LILIES grow** through the means of such a favorable **UNITY**—by which I mean under the particular auspices of **JESUS CHRIST**, who gathers and reunites all that belongs to him, as **OUR LILIES** do principally in their quality as **MOST CHRISTIAN** <note>The kings of France held the traditional title "Most Christian King" (*Rex Christianissimus*).</note>. **THEN** under the Protection of **THE CHURCH Our MOTHER**, who is uniquely **ONE**, and who favors them as her **Eldest sons** through **Distinguished** graces to further cement and increase this **UNITY**. Finally, under the most excellent **FORM** of **Political Government**, which, taking the name of **MONARCHY** from the **Unity** of its **Leader**, has as its **Examples** the **Sovereign Governments** of **THE UNIVERSE** and of **CHRISTENDOM**, both of which are ruled by **THE POWER** of a single **MAJESTY**, in order to unite **THE SPIRITS** of the faithful in **THE CHURCH**, and of the **People** in a **KINGDOM**, just as the **SPECIES** and **Virtues** of things do in **THE WORLD**.

This is what is magnificently suggested to us, both by **THE HOLINESS OF INNOCENT XI** <note>Pope Innocent XI, who reigned from 1676 to 1689.</note> happily seated today upon **THE CHAIR of SAINT PETER**, and by **THE AUGUST MAJESTY of LOUIS XIV** <note>Louis XIV, the "Sun King" of France.</note> in his **Glorious and Triumphant Reign**.

->**HERE ARE BOTH SEATED, EACH ON HIS OWN SIDE UPON HIS THRONE, ATTACHED INSEPARABLY TO UNITY; FOR**<-
->**AS A SINGLE ONE COMES FROM A SINGLE ONE; LIKEWISE TO A SUBJECT, A SINGLE ONE;**<-

Who could, however, suffice for several others, and who, with a fixed and attentive gaze upon **JESUS CHRIST**, seem to study in **Him** most fittingly the **LAWS of THE UNIVERSE**, which breathe nothing but **UNITY**, in order to happily govern **Each HIS OWN STATE UPON THIS DIVINE ORIGINAL**. **Moreover**, we owed **SPECTATORS** of this **CHARACTER** to **Our UNIVERSE**, and we owed **Our UNIVERSE** to **Spectators** of this **RANK**. We owed **THESE SPECTATORS** to **Our UNIVERSE**, since we have nothing more **Illustrious** in **THE WORLD** to uphold the merit of this **WORK** and make it recommendable; and **We** owed **Our UNIVERSE** to **These Spectators**, since having been born under their **Reign**, and being entirely for **JESUS CHRIST**, because it is entirely **CHRISTIAN**, it could belong more closely to **No Other** than **TO THE LEGITIMATE VICAR OF JESUS CHRIST AND TO THE MOST CHRISTIAN KING**.

Since the Permissions and Approvals are on the Latin Plate, I have contented myself with placing here the sentiments of several scholars regarding this work, after having read them from their letters written to the Author; I have been unable to give them in their entirety to the Public as I would have wished, due to the small amount of space remaining here. But as soon as the opportunity arises, I shall not fail to do so, as much for the honor due to the merit of these Illustrious Persons as for the glory of our Author.

->Caumartin. Made this.<- <note>original: *Fecit*</note>

This consists of **OUR IDEHOLOSOPHISCIE <term>Ideholosophiscie: A word coined by the author, likely combining the Greek roots *idea* (form/pattern), *holos* (whole/universal), and *sophia* (wisdom) to mean a "vision of universal wisdom."</term>, OR THE IDEAL SHADOW OF UNIVERSAL WISDOM, Catholic-Christian and Christian-Kabbalistic**; I leave it now, **MY DEAR READER**, to the illumination of an enlightened man like yourself, and to the sincerity of your good faith, to judge by this **PREAMBULAR INTRODUCTION** a thing that is clear enough, it seems to me, to the eyes of those who consider it with some knowledge. If this **IDEA** appears to you—as it already has to some—to be not entirely unreasonable, give thanks to **JESUS CHRIST CRUCIFIED**, and attribute the glory to Him as the **AUTHOR** of all good, while awaiting a more ample **EXPLANATION** of this **WORK**. **THE WORK** appears today as a posthumous publication through the care and diligence of the **Reverend Father FRANÇOIS-MARIE of Paris**, Capuchin Preacher, most affectionate friend of **THE AUTHOR**. — **IN PARIS. 1678.**

### Sentiments Of Several Scholars regarding the Work OF REVEREND FATHER ESPRIT, Taken from Their Letters Written To the Author.

**MY REVEREND FATHER**, **YOUR PHILOSOPHICAL FIGURE** has appeared so beautiful to us... and we have found in your design the nature of things married so skillfully with Art, and all your thoughts expressed by terms so proper and elegant, that one might say you alone embrace that which is treated in the works of all others who have undertaken to facilitate the Sciences for men, without reducing the extent of their Object; and who have proposed to join speculation with practice.

In your method, the Understanding is satisfied by subtle abstractions, which reveal even the most hidden circumstances of the subjects it wishes to consider: the Will is fulfilled by the direction it receives toward its legitimate Object: And the Memory is relieved by means of the order and economy that reign throughout this beautiful work. You have even found ways to represent your thoughts through **Hieroglyphs** <term>Hieroglyphs: In the 17th century, this term was often used to describe symbolic diagrams, emblems, or visual allegories rather than Egyptian script.</term> so agreeable to the Sight and the Imagination, that it seems you have spiritualized Colors and Figures to express the most elevated things, and those furthest from the senses: so that even the least subtle persons can discover, with this help, what would be inconceivable to them by any other way. Thus, My Reverend Father, we do not doubt that if your work is made public... it...

...May it be a great help in clarifying and facilitating the sciences, and we hope that through a prompt printing we will have the means to benefit more at leisure from all these lights. Meanwhile, we remain, **MY REVEREND FATHER**,
->Your very humble and very obedient<-
->servants **FORMAGET, MARIGNER**<-
*Paris, May 22, 1668.*

I confess quite frankly, **MY REVEREND FATHER**, that when you showed me your **MAP** <note>original: "CARTE"</note> yesterday, I immediately recalled that ladder which Jacob saw in his sleep: it was placed on the Earth, the top touched Heaven: the angels of GOD went up and down it, and THE LORD was leaning upon it... Your work is a ladder of all the sciences and all the arts: it is an abridgement of everything that can be known by Man, and everything that the human mind is capable of understanding. In this work, which can be called Very Great, **FATHER ESPRIT** <note>Father Esprit Sabbathier, the author. His name "Esprit" means "Spirit" or "Mind" in French, a pun the writer uses throughout to praise his intellect.</note> is all Spirit: and there will be no Man, whom **GOD** has formed just a little lower than the Angels, who cannot go up and down by this ladder; one can learn there everything that must, and can be known. You have arranged it so that it is the support of **JESUS CHRIST**, and that he is Truly its Beginning and its End... It is not enough to say that in this work—Grammar, Mythology, Rhetoric, Prayer, and History; Poetry, Logic, Arithmetic, and Algebra; Music, Geometry, Geography, Hydraulics, Optics, Astronomy, Astrology, and all other parts of Mathematics; Morals <note>Ethics</note>, Economics, Politics, Physics, Metaphysics; the faculties of Jurisprudence <note>Law</note>, Medicine, and all of Theology; Policing, the State, the Church, and Religion are yet but parts of what you have done: for all powers, both secular and spiritual, and Divine, which you have so perfectly represented there, find everything that can belong to them. To say it all in fewer words, you have made only one single thing of all things, and having your single **TABLE**, one has everything. I pray you, **MY REVEREND FATHER**, not to deny this work to the Public: everyone will undoubtedly ask it of you for their use: it is only just that Father Esprit <note>Spirit</note> gives spirit to the whole world: serve as a help both to those who begin to study and to those who are most learned. It will be a Method to some, and a kind of Reminiscence <note>The Platonic idea of "recalling" innate knowledge</note> to others. Make public then, if you please, that which will make you... a similar reward... and you will be blessed in every corner of the world. Farewell.
->**DE GOMONT,**<-
->**The Advocate.**<-

### Excerpt from the King's Privilege.

**BY** Grace and Privilege **OF HIS MAJESTY** it is permitted to **REVEREND FATHER FRANÇOIS MARIE** of Paris, Capuchin Preacher, to have engraved and printed a Work Entitled **THE IDEAL SHADOW OF GENERAL WISDOM &c.** <note>original Latin title: "IDEALIS UMBRA SAPIENTIAE GENERALIS"</note> with its explanation, the whole invented and composed by the late **REVEREND FATHER ESPRIT SABBATHIER**, Capuchin Preacher, by such engraver and printer as he shall see fit, during the span of ten years; and it is otherwise forbidden to all others, of whatever quality and condition they may be, to engrave or have engraved, to print or have printed, to sell, have sold, or distribute the said Work, or its explanation, in whole or in part, under any pretext of addition, change, or reduction, during the said time, without the consent of the said **REVEREND FATHER FRANÇOIS MARIE**, or of those who shall have rights from him, under penalty of three thousand Livres <note>a very large fine, equivalent to several years' wages for a skilled laborer</note> fine, and other penalties set forth at greater length by the said Privilege. Given at Versailles the 21st day of October, 1677. <insert>I have read a work entitled Ideal Shadow of General Wisdom by the Author Reverend Father Esprit Sabbathier, Capuchin Friar, done this 10th of Oct 1677. M. GR.</insert>
->**BY THE KING** in his Council,<-
->Signed **JEANNIN.**<-
Registered in the Book of the Community of Booksellers and Printers of Paris, October 27, 1677. By **E. COUTEROT**, Syndic.
And the said **REVEREND FATHER FRANÇOIS MARIE** has ceded and transferred the right of the present Privilege **TO MADEMOISELLE JABLIER** his sister, for her to enjoy during the said time, on the condition of paying the costs, according to the agreement made between them on February 9, 1678. **Finished printing for the first time April 10, 1679.**

<summary>This page contains introductory endorsements and the legal royal copyright (Privilege) for Father Esprit Sabbathier’s "Ideal Shadow of General Wisdom," a massive 17th-century attempt to map all human knowledge. The text uses the biblical metaphor of Jacob's Ladder to describe the work's educational structure, which spans from basic grammar to divine theology.</summary>
<keywords>Esprit Sabbathier, Jacob's Ladder, universal knowledge, 17th-century science, Royal Privilege, Capuchins, pedagogical method, classification of arts and sciences</keywords>
